samāso’yaṃ homavacanaḥ |
 homasyaivānyapadārthatvāt | upasarjanārthavidhānenāpi guṇavidhirbhavatyeva yathā hiraṇyastraja ṛtvijo

katham ?
 uttaraṃ—naivātra sāmānādhikaraṇyena prayojanaṃ, karmavacanataivātra na pratīyata

iti guṇavidhireva |

saṃskāro guṇo vidhīyate | etadevāha—yatrāghāryadravyaṃ
 upāṃśujāje tatraivedamāghāraṇaṃ

vidhīyata iti | nanu ca āghāryadravyako yāgaḥ āhavanīyasādhyatvāt prāptāghāraka eva |

sarve yajatayaḥ tiṣṭhadvomāḥ āhavanīyasādhyāḥ āhavanīye ca homa iti dīrghadhārākṣaraṇakriyā

āghāryadravyake yāge prāptaiveti kimāghāravidhānena ? aprāptaprāpakatvādvidheḥ | uttaraṃ

—homāghārādāghārāntaramidaṃ
 dravyasaṃskārāntaraṃ dravyasaṃskārārthamityabhiprāyaḥ |

evāghāryasaṃskāro’yaṃ ata evopāṃśuyāje

 nanu cāghārayatītyāghāraṇānuvādena sāntatyādividhiparāṇyetāni vākyānīti kathamebhya āghāraṇasyaiva prāptiḥ |

āghāravidhisannidhyāmnātānāṃ guṇaparatvāvagamāt | atrocyate — ānarthakyaṃ tāvadāghāraṇavidheraparihāryam | tathā hi mābhūdasau, tadabhāve santatādivākyānyeva viśiṣṭāghāraṇavidhāyakāni

santu, na vidhivākyam | na ca vācyaṃ santatādivākyānāmāghāraṇavidhiparatve bhedaḥ prāpnotīti,

tasmiṃstuṃ

 prāptamāghāraṇamaśritya sāntatyādividhiparāṇyetāni

vākyāni bhavanti iti | bhāṣyakāreṇa tvatra dravyavidhimāśritya pūrvapakṣitam | caturgṛhītamityanena

ca dravyasya prāptatvāt rāddhāntitam | tatra tāvat kathamupāṃśuyāje caturgṛhītaṃ vā etadityanenājyavidhiḥ na hyāghāraśabdenopāṃśuyājaḥ śakyate vaktum | athāghāryadravyakatvāt āghāra

ityucyate itaretarāśrayaṃ prāpnoti | ucyate, caturgṛhītaṃ vā etadityanenedamupadiśyate ājyena

caturgṛhītena karma kuryāditi | tacca dravyaśūnyatvādupāṃśuyāja evāvatiṣṭhate | tasya cājyadravyakatve sati āghāraśabdaṃ evārthaprāptānuvādaka ityadoṣaḥ | etacca bhāṣyakāreṇetthamuktam

yadi dravyavidhinā vā āghāraṇadravyasaṃskāravidhinā vā pūrvapakṣyate tathāpi śāstrāntareṇa vidheḥ

guṇavidhipakṣe darvihoma evaitadvidhānam

ca sāntatyavidhiḥ darvihomaṃ upāṃśuyājaṃ copasaṃkrāmataḥ | tataśca tābhyāmeva vihitatvāt agnihotraṃ

āghāramiti ca (na) guṇavidhirupapadyata iti saṃbaddhamevoktamiti bhāvaḥ | etameva bhāvamabudhdvā

 darvihomopāṃśuyājayorguṇavidhānāt | atra

 kāryaṃ pravṛttyātmakaṃ, nimittaṃ ca nāmātmakamiti virodhaḥ

syāt | guṇavidhipakṣe tu sa nāstīti śreyān guṇavidhiḥ | nanu ca
 guṇavidhipakṣe matvarthalakṣaṇā

aśabdārthabhūtā āśrayaṇīyetyu
ktamudbhidadhikaraṇe | pariharati—saiva nāstītyucyate |

kathaṃ nāsti ?
 uttaraṃ—ākhyātārtho hi kāryakāraṇasaṃbandhepyekarūpo’vagamyate |
 yadā

kāryatayāvagamyate yadā ca kāraṇatayā tadā ākhyātārtha ekenaiva rūpeṇāvagamyate; sādhyamāna eva

hi sādhana
mākhyātārtho(na)bhavati | ubhayoravagatau kimiti matvarthalakṣaṇā
 vyadhikaraṇatayā saṃbandhāvagamāt | udbhidā yāgaḥ kāryaḥ tena ca yāgena paśava iti | tasmādupapanno’yamarthaḥ vājapeyena yāgaḥ yāgena svārājyamiti
 na matvarthalakṣaṇā | natvevaṃ sati spaṣṭo
[p. 226]

vākyabheda ityatrāha—darśanato’nyathābhidhānam |
 paramārthatastu sādhyasādhanobhayarūpaḥ

sakṛdevākhyātenocyate | tadāha—na śabdārthāvagatau bheda
pratītiḥ | nanu ca vastudharmo’yaṃ

sādhyamāna eva hi sādhanaṃ bhavati iti na śabdādharmaḥ
 yāgena svārājyamiti na śabdaḥ ubhayarūpāvasthāmabhidhātuṃ śaknotītyarthaḥ | anyathā hi vidheyatayā’vagamayati śabdaḥ

anyathā coddeśyatayā |
 pariharati—satyaṃ sa tūbhayarūpatvāt tathā’bhidheyatā ca |

ubhayābhidhānepi yajeteti śabdaḥ sambandhinoḥ samarpayituṃ kṣamaḥ
 ubhayarūpatvāt |

sādhyasādhanobhayarūpatvāt yāgasya yathābhūta evāsau tathābhūtasyaiva śabdenābhidhānāt |

yadyapyubhayarūpo’bhihitaḥ tathāpi ubhayoḥ phalaguṇayoḥ sambandhinoḥ
 kṛte saṃbandhitvena

samarpayituṃ
 sa tu yajeteti śabdaḥB. adds sambandhinoḥ samarpayituṃ
 kṣamaḥ | kṣamaścet siddho guṇavidhiḥ

prāmāṇyaṃ ca
 siddham | rāddhāntamāha—maivaṃ; tau tu saṃbandhinau phalaguṇau yāgena saṃbandhumakṣamau

yataḥ na hi phalaṃ sādhyasādhanobhayātmakena saṃbandhuṃ kṣamam | nāpi guṇaḥ, phalasya sādhanamātrasaṃbandhitvāt | guṇasya ca sādhyamātrasambandhitvāt | tayoścetsambandhuṃ sāmarthyaṃ
 nāsti

[p. 227]

kimabhidhānasāmarthyaṃ kariṣyati |
 tasmāt pratyekaṃ sambandhayogyatayā bhedenātrābhidhānam |

tataśca dvayorarthayorvidhānāt
 vākyasya kāryadvayamidaṃ prāpnoti | yajeta svārājyakāmaḥ

yajeta vājapeyena iti parasparāsambaddhama |
 yadyevaṃ kiṃ jātaṃ ? uttaraṃ—parasparasaṃbandhāvagatistyaktā bhavati | anavagamyamānaścārthaḥ kalpitobhavati | tadevedaṃ sāpekṣatvamāyātam |

na hyanyathā śabdānavagamyārthakalpanopapadyate | nanu ca matvarthalakṣaṇāśrayaṇenaikavākyatvamityatrāha—viruddhā ca matvarthalakṣaṇā | tasmāduktābhiryuktibhiḥ yā udbhidadhikaraṇe tatprakhyādhikaraṇe

tadvyapadeśādhikaraṇe ca yuktayaḥ uktāḥ tābhiryuktibhiḥ nāmadheyatvapakṣaṃ
 eva yuktaḥ | yat punaḥ

vākye kāryanimittayorasāmañjasyaṃ uktaṃ tannāsti | avighātāt |
 nimittasya kāryāvighātānnāsāmañjasyam | nanu cānaupayikatvepyasāmañcasyameveti tatrāha—vighāte cāsāmañjasyaṃ

nānaupayikatve |
 yadyapi pravṛttyaupayikatvaṃ nāmno nāsti tathāpyasāmañjasyaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ |

tasmādadoṣaḥ |
 nāsti kāryanimittavirodhalakṣaṇo doṣaḥ

[p. 228]

tadguṇāstu || kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamārabhyate ?
 ekadeśyuttaramāha—ko’yaṃ praśnaḥ |

nanu cāgneyādayo guṇavidhayaḥ na nāmeti jñātum |
 ākṣeptā dūṣayati—yuktaṃ yaugikaprāptārtharūḍhīnāṃ nāmadheyatvaṃ, na cāgneyo’ṣṭākapāla ityatra nāmadheyatvaṃ sambhavati |
 kuta

ityāha—ākhyātārthasāmānādhikaraṇyāddhi nāmatoktā |
 ākhyātārthavidhipare vākye arthāntarasya vidheyatvāyogāt padāntaramapyākhyātārthaparamevetyanena hetunā nāmatoktā | bhavatvevaṃ

tathāpi kimityāha—na cātra bhavatītyākhyātamapyavidheyārthatvāt bhāvārtham |
 yadetadbhavatītyākhyātaṃ na tadbhāvārtham | kasmādbhāvārthaṃ na bhavatīti-atrocyate | avidheyārthatvāt |

yadi bhavatyartho’pi bhāvarūpaḥ kimityasau vidheyarūpo na bhavatīti na cāsau vidheyaḥ,
 tenāvidheyatvādavagacchāmo na bhāvārthatvamiti | B. abhāvārthatve tadvidhiparatvābhāvādvākyasya tatsāmā etc.
bhāvārthatve hi vidheyaparatvādvākyasya tatsāmānādhikaraṇyādāgneyaśabdasya nāmatopapattiḥ | na coktāddhetorvidhīyamānākhyātārthasāmānādhikaraṇyarūpādanyaddhetvantaraṃ nāmadheyatve’
stītyanenābhiprāyeṇāsmākamayaṃ praśnaḥ
 kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamārabhyata iti mandatvāt pūrvapakṣasya nedamadhikaraṇabhārabdhavyamityarthaḥ | samādhātā āha
[p. 230]

—atrocyate—punarapi sāpekṣataivātra
 śaṅkyate | pūrvapakṣe codanāyāḥ sāpekṣatvamāśaṅkya

siddhānte’napekṣatvaṃ vaktumidamadhikaraṇamārabhyataB. ārabdhavyamityarthaḥ
 ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ sāpekṣatā āśaṅkyate ?

uttaraṃ—yathaiva nāmadheyatvaṃ na sambhavati bhāvārthasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvāt tathā guṇavidhānamapi bhāvārthāśravaṇāt |
 bhāvārthāśrito hi guṇavidhiriti | na cātra bhāvārthaḥ śrūyate

yadāśrito’yaṃ guṇavidhiḥ syāt | yadā guṇavidhitvaṃ nāmatvaṃ ca na saṃbhavati tadā gatyantarābhāvāt

prasaktamaprāmāṇyam | tacca śabdasya na svābhāvikaṃ, api tu pauruṣeyatvakṛtam

A. pauruṣeyatvena
 | ataśca

pauruṣeyatvāpatteḥ prasaktā pramāṇāntarāpekṣatā kṛtsnasya vedarāśeḥ | nanvāgneya iti dravyadevatāsaṃyogaśravaṇāt
 tasya ca yajyarthamantareṇānupapatteḥ yajyartho bhāvārthaḥ
 tataśca tatra

nāmatā guṇavidhānaṃ vā saṃbhavatyeva | kathamaprāmāṇyaṃ ? pūrvapakṣyāha—satyaṃ yadi dravyadevatā

sambandhaḥ śrūyate | ākṣeptā āha-nanu cāgneya iti śrūyate |
 pūrvapakṣī vadati—satyaṃ yadyaṣṭākapālaviṣayo na syāt |
 aṣṭākapālasya hi āgneyā śrūyate | na cāsau saṃbhavatītyaśruta

prāya eva dravyadevatāsaṃbandhaḥ | ākṣeptā pṛcchati—yadyaṣṭākapālaviṣaya eva
 dravyadevatāsaṃbandhaḥ1 syāt tataḥ
 kiṃ sambhavatyeva | aṣṭākapālasyāpyāgneyatvamiti bhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣyuttaramāha—idaṃ tato bhavati avihitaivāṣṭākapālatā pratipattavyā |
 uddeśyatvāt |

na cāvihito’ṣṭākapālo’sti | nanu ca yo’ṣṭasu kāpaleṣu śrṛtaḥ so’ṣṭākapālaḥ
 lokika

uddeśyo bhaviṣyati vācaniko vā kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanāt |
 asyārthaḥ—atha

vā laukika uddeśyo mā bhavatu kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanāt | kintu vācanika

evāstu | sarvathā tāvadanenaiva vacanenaivāvihitasyāpyāgneyatā vidhātuṃ śakyā |

pūrvapakṣī dūṣayati—vācanikastāvanna saṃbhavati | aṣṭasaṅkhyāśravaṇāt |
 na hi vācanike

aṣṭasaṃkhyāvidhānamantareṇāṣṭākapālatā labhyate | sāpi saṅkhyā vidhīyata iti cet | dūpayati

—na itaretarāśrayaprasasaṅgāt | āgneye saṅkhyā; saṅkhyāyāmāgneyaḥ iti |
 āgneyānuvādenā

’ṣṭasaṅkhyā vidheyā-āgneyo yaḥ soṣṭākapālaḥ kartavya iti | tathā yo’ṣṭākapālaḥ sa āgneya

B. devatāsamyogaḥ

[p. 232]

iti | aṣṭākapālānuvādenāgneyateti | tasmānna guṇavidhiḥ sambhavati | na ca nāma
 saṃbhavatītyuktam |
 tasmādaprāmāṇyam | yadyaprāmāṇyābhiprāyeṇa pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ kathaṃ tarhi nāmeti bhāṣyakāreṇoktam ?
 samādhātā āha—kvo voktam |
 ākṣeptā āha—uktamupanyāse kiṃ

nāmadheyamuta guṇavidhiriti |
 sandehakāla upanyastam | tatra nāmadheyamiti pūrvapakṣavacanavyaktiḥ guṇavidhiriti rāddhāntavacanavyaktiḥ tathā pūrvapakṣaparigrahasamaye guṇavidhipratiṣedhaṃ

ca kurvatā
 pāriśeṣyāt nāmadheyamityuktaṃ bhavati |
 samādhatte—satyamuktaṃ nāmadheyatvaṃ na

tu vidheyasya
 bhāvārthasyedaṃ nāmateti noktamityarthaḥ | kiṃ tarhyuktamityāha—nāmagrahaṇāt

prasiddhārthatā pradarśitā |
 āgneyapadaṃ vidheyārthaṃ na bhavati | kintu prasiddhārthamityuktam

nāmadheyasyāpi prasiddhārthatvāt | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kathaṃ sāpekṣatoktetyatrāha—prasiddhiśca

pramāṇāntarādṛte na sambhavatīti sāpekṣatvaṃ prayogasya darśitam |
 rāddhāntamāha—syādetadevaṃ yadi saṅkhyāsambandhe devatāsambandhe ca itaretarāśrayatā syāt | B. na cāsāvasti
nanvetadasti |

kathaṃ nāsti ? uttaraṃ—aṣṭākapālasya hi devatāvidhiḥ |
 pūrvapakṣaṃ smārayati—nanu cāsāva
[p. 233]

prasiddha ityuktam | rāddhāntī vadati—kimabhidheyatayāpi na prasiddhaḥ |
 abhidheyatayā

prasiddha ityarthaḥ | abhidhānaprasiddhiṃ cāśritya vidhānamavakalpate | pūrvapakṣī vadati—na
 abhidheyatayā, prasiddhaḥ kintu kāryatayā |
 aṣṭākapālo hi kārya iti kṛtvā’prasiddhatvamucyate |

rāddhāntyāha—iṣṭamevaita
tkāryatayā’prasiddhatvam | yata eva kāryatayā’prasiddhatvaṃ ata eva

vidhīyate |
 upasaharati—tasmādabhidhānāprasiddhirvidhānaṃ vihanti | na punaḥ kāryatayā’prasiddhiḥ | tasmādyajyarthasyeva siddhamaṣṭākapālārthasyāpi vidhānam | 1prasiddhābhidhānagamyatvaṃ ca darśitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa nānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti
 vadatā | aprasiddhārthatve hi na hyanyenāpi śrapitasya grahaṇaṃ prasaktam, yannirākaraṇāya yujyate vaktum | kapāleṣu

śrapayatīti vacanāt nānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti | tasmādyajyarthavat taddhitārtho’pyayaṃ

prasiddhaḥ |
 yathā yatra yajeteti sākṣācchravaṇe yajyarthasyābhidhānataḥ prasiddhiḥ tathā taddhitārthasyāpītyarthaḥ | ato na sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ
 guṇaviśiṣṭakarmavidhānenānapekṣatopapatteḥ |

na cātra prāptārthatā agnihotravadaṣṭā
kapālatāyāḥ saṃbhavati
 iti sāpi vidhīyate yathā

agnihotraṃ juhotītyatrāgnirdevatā na vidhīyate agnidevatāyāḥ praptatvāt tatheha na saṃbhavatīti |

C. prasiddhārthābhidhānaṃ cedaṃ

[p. 234]

barhirājyayoḥ |
 kā punaravāntarasaṅgatirityatrāha—prāsaṅkimidamadhikaraṇam |

kaḥ punaratra prasaṅgaḥ ? uttaraṃ—vidheyenāpyarthena nāmārthānvayo dṛṣṭaḥ aṣṭākapālamiti |

ayamapi cājyaśabdaḥ karmanimittaka eva loke dṛṣṭaḥ |
 saṃskāranimitto’yamājyaśabdo loke

dṛśyate | saṃskṛtāvasthāyāmevājyaśabdaprayogāt | tasmādayamapi kāryānvayīA. kāryārthānvayitvamājyaśabdasya

 kāryatayā’nvayī | kāryānvayyājyaśabdāsyārthaḥ | etadeva darśayati—saṃskāreṇājyaṃ kuryāditi |

ājyaṃ vilayanena saṃskuryādityarthaḥ |
 nanu cākarmanimittakopyājyaśabdaḥ kvaciddeśaviśeṣe

dṛṣṭaḥ, ājyaṃ krayyaṃ, barhirādāya gāvo gatā iti |
 tena kathamekāntataḥ saṃskāranimittatā

śakyā’vasātum | pariharati—sārvabhaumo hi prayogaḥ śrauto bhavitumarhati | naikadeśaprabhavaḥ saṃskāranimittakaśca prayogaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ |
 A. ye saṃskṛte śabdaṃ prayuñjate teṣāṃ tāvatsaṃskāranimittatā
ye tāvadasati saṃskāre śabdaṃ na prayuñcate

teṣāṃ sphuṭaiva saṃskāranimittatā | ya’pi jātimātre prayuñcate te saṃskṛte’pi prayuñjate iti

saṃskṛtaviṣayatvaṃ sārvabhaumam | tadeva ca śrautam | ekadeśe yajjātibacanatvaṃ tadgauṇam |

kasmādevaṃ ? uttaraṃ—prasiddhasya
 hi padasya śraute’rthe gauṇatā saṃbhavati
 nāprasiddhasya |

yathaiva hi jvalane prasiddho’gniśabdo māṇavake tatsādṛśyena prayujyamāno gauṇo bhavati | sarveṣāṃ

saṃskāranimittatā prasiddheti jātimātre gauṇaḥ ekadeśabhavaḥ prayogaḥ | yeṣāṃ tu saṃskṛtamātra

eva prayogaḥ teṣāṃ prayogadvayābhāvānmukhyaḥ prayogaḥ | na cāyaṃ sarvatra ghṛtavacanatayā prasiddhaḥ |

aprasiddhasya ca ghṛte’nyatra gauṇatvaṃ nopapadyate |
 ye saṃskāranimittaṃ śabdaṃ manyante te

jātyantarepi śabdaṃ na prayuñjate tatra sa gauṇo na yuktaḥ anyatra mukhyaprayogādarśanāt | karmanimittatve
 yatra punaḥ ekadeśe ghṛte prayujyate
 asaṃskṛte’pi tatra karmanimittake’pi prayogāt

siddho
 gauṇaḥ, anyatra mukhyaprayogasiddheḥ siddho ghṛte gauṇaḥ | tasmāt saṃskāranimittatvādājyādiśabdānāṃ kāryārthataiva yuktā | atrocyate | kāryārthatayā’nvayo na ghaṭate itaretarāśratvaprasaṅgāt | barhiṣaḥ
 sataḥ prokṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇena ca barhiriti |
 yadvai prokṣaṇādinā

saṃskriyate tadbārhirityucyate | barhiruddeśena prokṣaṇādayo vidhīyanta iti na jñāyate kasya

prokṣaṇādayo vidhīyanta iti | yadyevamitaretarāśrayatvaṃ kathaṃ tarhyaṣṭākapāle
 netaretarāśrayaḥ ?

uttaraṃ—na cetaretarāśrayamabhidhānataḥ prasiddherityuktam | śha punaḥ kāryata evābhidhānaṃ,

tenetaretarāśrayatvamiti vaiṣamyam |
 pūrvapakṣī tvāha—yūpe kathaṃ ?
 yūpaṃ takṣatīti śrūyate |

yūpaśabdaśca kāryasaṃskāravacanaḥ tatra vyaktamitaretarāśrayatvam | anyataḥ prasiddhyabhāvāt

agatikā gatiriyam |
 yathā barhirādāvanyataḥ prasiddhirasti jātimātre prayogadarśanāt tathā

yūpe nāsti tena asati gatyantare eṣāgatirāśrīyate yattakṣaṇārhaṃ yattakṣaṇakarmabhūtaṃ tat takṣaṇena yūpaṃ

karotīti | keyamagatikā gatiḥ |
 ekagranthenāha—kiṃ na śāstrādevāvagamyate ?

ayamāśayaḥ—yadi śāstrāt avagamyeta tadā barhirādiṣvapi tathāstviti | uttaraṃ—satyāṃ

prasiddhau na śāstrādavagamyate yatrānyataḥ prasiddhirasti tatra sāmānyena vyavahāro na yuktaḥ |

viśeṣasyaivāvagamāditi tatreyaṃ śāstrādgatirnāvagamyate | yatprokṣaṇārhaṃ prokṣaṇe karmabhūtaṃ tadbārhiḥ

tat prokṣaṇena saṃskriyata iti | aprasiddhe punaḥ padārthe apratihatatvādvidhyarthasya kalpyate |
[p. 237]

anyathā vidhyarthapratighātaprasaṅgāt | yaduktaṃ sārvabhaumaprayogo gauṇo na saṃbhavati

śrautasyāprasiddheriti ekadeśasthastu
 gauṇassaṃbhavatīti atrocyate—nāvaśyaṃ prasiddhaśrautārthameva gauṇaṃ prayuñjate lokikaḥ
 rājaśabdamiva | yathā rājaśabdāmāryā aprasiddhaśrotārthamapi

gauṇaṃ prayuñjate tathā barhirādiśabdamapi | codayati—tatrāpi
 rājaśabde’pi tulyo doṣaḥ |

ekagranthenāha—vācyasādṛśyapratipattyarthaṃ hi gauṇaṃ prayuñjate | atadbuddhyāpi gauṇaḥ

prayujyata iti haṭho’yaṃ;
 vācyārthasādṛśyapratipādanāya hi gauṇaḥ śabdaḥ prayujyate | mukhye

cārthe’vijñāte na tatsādṛśyaṃ jñāyata iti sādṛśyabuddhāvasatyāṃ gauṇasya prayogo C. ’nimittakaḥ
nirhetukaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate—na prayokturvivakṣāmapekṣate śabdārthasaṃbandhaḥ | saṃbandhāpekṣā hi vivakṣā |

asyārthaḥ—yadyapi prayoktā gauṇabuddhyā prayuṅkte prayojyaśca tathaiva pratipadyate tathāpi pārśvastho

bālaḥ mukhyārthe prayogamadṛṣṭvā mukhyatāmevāvagacchati | sa tathā pratītya vyavahāramapi pravartayet |

tena prayoktṛvivakṣāṃ nāpekṣate pārśvasthasya śabdārthasaṃbandhaḥ, kintu yādṛśaṃ mukhyaṃ śabdārthasaṃbandhaṃ

pratipadyate tādṛśameva punarvyavahārakāle’pi pārśvastho vivakṣati | etadeva dṛṣṭāntapurassaraṃ
[p. 238]

darśayati—uktaṃ hi gopotalikādiṣu aśaktijatvāparijñānādanādiśabdamivaC. anādṛgiva
 prayuñjate | eva

matrāpi vivakṣā’parijñānāt kasyacit prayoktuḥ vivakṣāśūnyamarthāntare prayuñcate
 | jātiviṣaye mukhyaṃ śabdārthaṃ jānatā kenacidekadeśajātena gauṇaḥC. gauṇasaṃskāra
 saṃskārayoginyatajjātīye’pi

barhirādiśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | atra mukhyamanyamarthamaviduṣo gauṇārthavivakṣā’sti | evamajānato

mukhyabuddhiḥ pravṛttā | yathā gopotalikādiṣvanāditvabuddhiraśaktijatvamaviduṣaḥ tannibandhanaśca

śabdārthavyavahāraḥ | kathaṃ punaridaṃ vijñāyate
 bhrāntimūlo’yaṃ saṃskṛtaviṣaye mukhyaśabdavyavahāra

iti | uttaraṃ—loke vede ca vyavahāradarśanāt | vede tāvadājyaṃ vidhāya kvacit ghṛtaśabdena stauti
 tatra yadi saṃskāravacanaḥ ājyaśabdaḥ syāt tatra stutau ghṛtaśabdaḥ upalakṣaṇārthaḥ syāt | jātivacanatve tu svārthaḥ, tena jātivacanataiva vedālocanayā’vasīyate |

karmaṇāṃ punaśca tatsiddhaye sādhyasādhanatvaṃ viśeṣaniyamaśceti | anyatarānugṛhītapakṣe punaḥ

alpīyasyadṛṣṭānumānaprasaṅgakalpanā bhaviṣyatīti | kimidaṃ adṛṣṭānumānaprasaṅgakalpaneti ?

uttaraṃ—adṛṣṭamapramāṇakaṃ
 yatkilādṛṣṭaṃ tadapramāṇakaṃ anumānaṃ niyamānumānaṃ yasya āśrayāśrayitvaprasaṅgasya asau adṛṣṭānumānaprasaṅgaḥ adṛṣṭānumānaśvāsau prasaṅgaśceti tasya kalpanā alpīyasī |
[p. 285]

nanu ca naiva bhavati kalpanā kimidaṃ alpīyasīti ?
 pariharati—anyatarānugṛhītapakṣe’pi vidhyarthasyaiva
 vidheyaṃ vidhyarthaḥ tasyaivānugraha iti vidyate kalpanā |
 anugraho’pi

kriyamāṇo na svarūpamātrasyābhyupeyaḥ niyogārthavirodhāt | nanu ca śābdamevedaṃ kimatra

kalpyate
 ? pariharati—kāryato’vagamaṃ navame vakṣyāmaḥ
 apūrvaprayuktatvābhyupagamena | tena

kalpanāvācoyuktirghaṭate | yadi kāryato’vagatiḥ na tarhi śābdatvamityāśaṅkyāha—śabdāvagatakāryamūlatvāttu śābdam | tasmātsādhūktam evamalpīyasyadṛṣṭhānumānaprasaṅkalpanā

bhaviṣyatīti
 tasmādekamapūrvaṃ iti bhāṣyakāreṇopasaṃhṛtamākṣipati—kimidamekamapūrvamiti |

pariharati—nanvidameva,
 suvyakta evāyamarthaḥ | punarākṣipati—naivaṃG. mevaṃ
 ekasmādapūrvamiti

prakamāt |
 tasmādekasmādapūrvamityupasahartavyam | naikamapūrvamiti | atrābhidhīyate—yadā

pūrvapakṣe pratipadaṃ dharmaḥ tadā pratipadaṃ niyogabhedena bhavitavyam |
 kasmāt ?

uttaram—pratipadamanubandhabhedāt |
 yadā punaḥ parasparasaṃbaddhānāṃ vidhinā yogaG. vidhiniyogaḥ
;

tadā eka evānubandhaḥ | ataścaikamapūrvamityupasaṃhṛtam | nanvevamapi ekasmādapūrvamiti

kimiti nopasaṃhṛtam yena phaladvāreṇopasaṃhāraḥG. upasaṃhiṛyate
 ityāha-amumeva ca pūrvapakṣe kāryabhedaṃ darśayituṃ
[p. 286]

bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa phalenopasaṃhṛtam tasmādekamapūrvamiti,
 kāryabhedadarśanaṃ ca

pūrvapakṣeG. parasparāsaṃbandhā
 parasparasaṃbandhāvagatiḥ nirbījā prāpnotīti siddhāntayuktisiddhyartham | atra ca niyogārthatvāduccāraṇasya sarvapadānāṃ sākṣānniyogasaṃbandho bhavati | sākṣātsambandhasaṃbhave ca

paramparāśrayaṇānupapatteḥ sarveṣāṃ niyogaviṣayatvamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu sarveṣāṃ

niyogaviṣayatve parasparasaṃbandhāvagamavirodhāt pratipadaṃ niyogabhede bhinnaniyogānupraveśināṃ

sambandhānupapatteḥ yathāviniyogaṃ viṣayabhāvoG. viṣayabhedo
 nyāyya iti anyatarānugṛhītasyānyatarasya

viṣayatvam | parasparasaṃbandhāśrayaṇe ca (vi)niyogāvagamaG. niyogāvagatameva
eva pramāṇamiti |

yadā ekasmādapūrvaṃ tadā saṃdehaḥ | kiṃ bhāvaśabdebhyaḥ uta dravyaguṇaśabdebhya iti

atra pṛcchati—bhāvaśabdagrahaṇena kasya grahaṇaṃ ?
 ekadeśyuttaramāha—ākhyātānāmeva |

punaścodayati—evaṃ tarhi sandehaṃ darśayatā kiṃ
 nāmaśabdebhyaḥ utākhyātaśabdebhya iti

vaktavyam | kimidamucyate—kiṃ bhāvaśabdebhyaḥ uta dravyaguṇaśabdebhya iti |
 ākṣeptāraṃ
[p. 287]

pṛcchati—G. kiṃ tatra
kiṃ vātrātiricyate
 bhāṣyakārīyepi sandehopanyāse kiñcidadhikaṃ nāsti yena

lābhena kiṃ nāmaśabdebhya utākhyātaśabdebhya iti kartumucitamiti codyate | ākṣeptā’’ha

—dvirukto nāmaśabdo’
tiricyate | dravyaguṇaśabdagrahaṇe kriyamāṇe G. nākhyātaśabdānāṃ
nāmaśabdānāṃ śabdadvayenopasaṅgrahaḥ | bhāvaśabdagrahaṇeG. bhāvaśabdāśrayaṇe
 kriyamāṇe pratiyogitayā dravyaguṇagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam |

vācyāvacchedena hi tadā śabdo nirdiśyata iti bhinnau dravyaguṇaśabdau vācyau śabdopasthāpanāyoccārayitavyau | ākhyātagrahaṇe tu svarūpeṇa śabdanirdeśāt śabdasvarūpanirdeśako nāmaśabda
 eva prayoktavyo bhavati | tataśca śabdadvayānuccāraṇaṃ lāghabārtham | paramasamādhātā

āha—anabhiprāyajño hi bhavāt yadi hi kimākhyātaśabdebhyaḥ uta nāmaśabdebhya ityucyate

tataśca nirṇīyate
 ākhyātaśabdebhya evāpūrvamiti tadā āgneyo’ṣṭhākapāla ityakriyārthaḥ

syāt | dravyadevatāsambandho niyogaviṣayo na syāt nāmatvādāgneyaśabdasya; bhavatīti ca

bhavatyarthaḥ kriyārtho niyogaviṣayaḥ syāt ākhyātatvāt
 bhavatiśabdasya | aniṣṭaṃ caitadubhayamapi | bhavatyarthasyavidheyatvāt
 | na hi bhūto bhavatyartho niyoktuṃ śakyate amṛtaśca

svayamavidyamānatvāt | tena bhavatyartho na vidheyaḥ | dravyadevatāsaṃbandhasya ca vidheyatvāt |
[p. 288]

tasmāda
niṣṭāpattirmābhūditi nāmākhyātagrahaṇaṃ na kṛtaṃ
 bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa | kiṃ tarhi ?

yenaiva svabhāvenākhyātārthasya vidhyarthatā saṃbhavati yena ca svabhāvena nāmno
 vidhyarthatā

na saṃbhavati tenaiva svabhāvenopanyāsaḥ kṛto
 bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa | kena svabhāvenākhyātārthasya vidhyarthatā, kena vā nāmno na G. nivartiteti
saṃbhavatīti
 praśnadvaye yathā saṅkhyamuttaradvayam |

bhāvārthatayā ākhyātārthasya vidhyarthatā, dravyaguṇābhidhānatayā ca nāmno
 na vidhyarthatā |

yathā caitattathopariṣṭādvyākhyāsyāmaḥ vakṣyāmaḥ
 | atra bhāṣyam—kaḥ punarbhāvaḥ ke vā bhāvaśabdā

iti tadvyācaṣṭe—kaḥ punarayamākhyātārthavyatirikto bhāvo nāma ? kaścākhyātaśabdavyatirikto bhāvaśabdo nāma |
 uttarabhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—prativacanantu
 uttaraṃ tu yajeta dadātijuhotītyeṣāṃ
 yo’rthaḥ sa bhāvārthaḥ ete ca bhāvaśabdā iti nanu ca yāgadānahomaśabdā eta

iti parihārabhāṣyam | tadvyācaṣṭe—nanu sa evāyamākhyātārthaḥ ta evāmī ākhyātaśabdāḥ ityākṣepaḥ naitadevaṃ, yāgādiśabdāścaita

 iti parihārabhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—samādhānaṃ

tu nākhyātārthamātraṃ yajeta dadāti juhotītyatra
 gamyate kiṃ tarhi ? arthāntarotpādakatvamapi,
 karturanyo yo’rthaḥ tadutpādakatvamapi gamyate | na kevalaṃ pūrvāparībhāva evāvagamyate yathā bhavatīti |
 pūrvāparībhāvamātraṃ ākhyātamātrārthaḥG. ākhyātārthaḥ
 | na tathā bhāvayedityatraG. pacatītyatra

anyotpādakatvaṃ tu bhāvārthaḥG. bhāvārthatvaṃ
 tacca bhavatītyatra nāsti | kartureva tatrotpattyavagamāt | yadetaduktamasmābhiḥ taduktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇāpi yajyādyarthaścāto’vagamyate bhāvayediti ca
 ityanena

bhāṣyeṇa | nanvanya evārtho’sya bhāṣyasyeti matvā pṛcchati—kimuktamanena
 bhāṣyeṇa | uttaraṃ

—pūrvāparībhāvaścāto’bagamyate anyotpādakatvaṃ ceti
 tatra G. yatra kāryaścāto
yajyādyarthaścāto’vagamyata

ityanena pūrvāparībhūtayāgādyavagatiruktā | bhāvayedi
tyanyotpādakatvāvagatiḥ | punaścāyamartho

bhāṣyakāreṇa prakaṭīkṛtaḥ | tathā yateta yathā yatkiṃñcidbhavatīti |
 anyotpattyanuguṇaḥ puruṣaprayatno’vagamyata ityarthaḥ | tenaite ākhyātaśabdā api bhāvaśabdā ityucyante |
 yataḥ pūrvāparībhūtānyotpādakatvasvabhāvo yo’rthaḥ sa bhāvārthaḥ tadarthāśca śabdāḥ bhāvaśabdāḥ | ato dravyaguṇaśabdagrahaṇamapi |
 nāmaśabde hi kriyamāṇe pratiyogyartho na nirdiṣṭaḥ syāt nāmaśabdānāmapi keṣāṃ

cidbhāvārthatvāt | nanvevamapi yena svabhāvena kriyārthatvaṃG. akiyārthatvaṃ na ni
 tanna nirdiṣṭaṃ syāditi yadi kaścinmanyate
[p. 290]

taṃ pratyāha—dravyaguṇapratyayotpākatvādakriyārthatvaṃ na nāmatvāt |
 nāmnaḥ āgneya ityādeḥ

kriyārthatvadarśanāt | kiṃ tāvatprāptamaviśeṣeṇeti | ko’bhiprāyaḥ
 evaṃ vadato bhāṣyakārasya |

uttaraṃ—dravyaṃ vā bhavatu kriyārthaṃ kriyā vā bhavatu dravyārthā | sarvathā tāvadaparityaktakiyākārakasambandha eva kāryasaṃbandhaḥ |
 yadi kriyāviṣayo niyogaḥ tadā itarattadarthamiti

nyāyāt dravyaṃ kriyārtham | yadā tu dravyaṃ niyogaviṣayaḥ tadā kriyā dravyārthā | pakṣadvayepi

cāsmin kriyādravyārthapakṣe kriyākārakasambandho na virudhyate | tenāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvādanyatarasya

vidhiviṣayatvam | na caivamanadhyavasāyenāprāmāṇyam | anuṣṭhānasyādhyavasitatvāt | ekarūpameva

hi pakṣadvayeṣyanuṣṭhānaṃ anuṣṭhānānadhyavasāyena cāprāmāṇyam | atrocyate | naivaṃ sidhyati

kriyākārakasaṃbandhaḥ | yadā hi kriyā dravyārthā
 tadā dravyaviṣayo niyogaḥ tadā kriyākārakasambandhastyakta eva bhavati | kasmāt ?
 uttaraṃ—dravyaṃ hi tadā karmabhūtaṃ kriyāyāṃ

pradhānabhūtaṃ dravyaṃ, kriyayā īpsyamānatvāt | īpsyamānaśca karmāpadyeta | na ca karma kārakaṃ

kārakasthā hyutpattiḥ karmeti vakṣyāmaḥ
 | kārakaM. kārakasthā
sya yā avasthotpadyate sā karma na kārakāvasthaiva |

yadi karma kārakaṃ na bhavati kathaṃ tarhi karmaṇi kārake dvitīyetyāgamaḥ | tathā karturīpsita-

[p. 291]

tamaṃ karmeti ca kārakādhikāre
 | etadāgamaparyālocanayāpi karmaṇaḥ kārakatvamavagamyate karmaṇi

kārake dvitīyeti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśāt | tathā īpsitatamaṃ yatkārakaṃ tatkarmetyabhidhānāt |

samādhatte—yatra kriyāphalamabhiniṣpadyate
 tat kārakaṃ karmeti sūtrārthaḥ
 | kriyāphalabhāgitvaṃ

kārakasya karmatvaṃ; na punaḥ kriyāniṣpādakatvaṃ kriyāniṣpādakatayā ca kārakatvaṃ nānyathā | kasmādevaṃ

vidhaḥ sūtrārthaḥ ? ityatrāha—anyathāhīpsitatamagrahaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt
 | na hi kriyāniṣpādakaṃ

kriyayā īpsitatamaṃ bhavati | kriyājanyaphalabhāgitvāvasthā ca kriyayā īpsitatamā bhavati |

tasmāt karmaṇi kārake dvitīyetyapi siddham
 | avasthādvayayogitvāt | tasmāt

kriyārthaṃ
 niyogārthaṃ niyogaviṣayasamarpakaṃ yadā’’khyātaṃ
 bhavati tadaiva dravyaguṇayoḥ kārakatvasiddhiḥ kārakatvena ca
 tayoḥ śravaṇaṃ; naitadanugrahāya ākhyātārthasyaiva niyogārthatvaṃ

niścīyate ata āha—ye bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdāstebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeta yajetetyevamādibhyaḥ |

atra sūtrakāreṇa bhāvārthebhyaḥ kiyā pratīyeteti vadatā hetvantarānupādānāt bhāvārthatvameva heturukta

iti matvā pṛcchati—kutaḥ iti |
 tadvyācaṣṭe—kuta iti praśnaḥ G. kimatra
kimanyathā bhāvārthatvaṃ hīyeta

tadapi bhāvārthasyāniyogaviṣayatve bhāvārthatvameva hīyeta tadā syādayaṃ hetuḥ na caitadastīti bhāvaḥ |
[p. 292]

uttaraṃ—bāḍhaṃ hīyate pūrvāparībhāve hi bhāvārthatvaṃ bhavati
 bhāvaśabdasya pūrvāparībhāvavācitvāt | tathāpi kimityāha—sa ca pūrvāparībhāvo’tra dravyaguṇayoḥ tadarthatve sati

bhavati | anyathā hi godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayeditivadāśrayāvagatireva syāt | na

ca godohanasādhyaṃ praṇayanamavasīyate
 | asyārthaḥ-sādhyatve hi bhāvārthatvaṃ bhavati bhāvārthasya

ca niyogaviṣayatve dravyaguṇayośca tadarthatve sādhyatvaṃ bhāvārthasya sampadyate | dravyaguṇavostu

niyogārthatve āśrayamātratayaivāranmātārthavatvamavagataṃR. khyātārthānāmavagatiḥ
 syāt na sādhyatvena godohanena paśukāmasya

praṇayeditivat | nanu ca godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayedityapi pūrvāparībhūtameva praṇayanaṃ

pratīyate | godohanaṃ ca
 karaṇameva | ato’siddho dṛṣṭāntaḥ | pariharati—satyaṃ pratīyate na

punaḥ godohanena praṇayedityevadvākyārthatayā,
 asminvākye godohanasya vākyārthatvāt tadupakārakatvācca praṇayanasya viparītaḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvo’vagamyate | laukikastvayaṃ kriyākārakabhāvaḥ

śabdenopādīyate | tasmādaniyamapakṣe na niyamena kriyākārakabhāvamajahato vidhyarthatvaṃ

vākyasya pratīyate
 | yadā tāvat kriyāniyogārthā tadā pratīyamānakiyākārakabhāvāparityāgaḥ | dravyaguṇayostu kiyārthatvena uktena nyāyena kiyākārakabhāvaparityāga eveti

nāniyamaḥ | api tu bhāvārtha eva niyogārtha iti | ata idamuktaṃ bhāvārthatvādeveti |
[p. 293]

etaduktaṃ bhavati—evameva saṃbandhāvagamāditi |
 bhāvārthasya niyogārthatayā sādhyatvaṃ

dravyaguṇayostu sādhanatvamevetyevamevaG. tyeva
 sambandho’vagamyate, nānyathetyarthaḥ | tadidānīṃR. tamidānīṃ

pūrvāparībhūtabhāvavyatirekiṇamarthaṃ bhāvaśabdavācyaṃ prapañcayati
—yatpratipādanāyākhyātaśabdopādānamakṛtvā bhāvārthagrahaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ taṃ pūrvāparībhūtārthavyatirekiṇaṃ anyotpādakatvamarthaṃ prapañcayati

—yaḥ āhuḥ kimupi bhāvayediti te svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti brūyuriti |

atra cedamabhimataṃ—liṅādiyuktavākyaṃśravaṇasamanantarabhāvinīṃ pravṛttimavagamya pravartakārthābhidhāyitā liṅādīnāmavasīyate | kāryasya ca pravartakatvāvagamāt tadeva liṅādayo’pyavabodhayantīti

niścīyate | kāryaṃ ca kṛtisādhyaṃ kṛtipradhānamucyate | taduktam | kṛtisādhyaṃ pradhānaṃ

yat tatkāryamabhidhīyate
 iti | tasya cābhidhānaṃ kṛtimanabhidhāya nopapadyate iti liṅādayaḥ

kṛtimapyabhidadhati | kṛtireva ca bhavituḥ prayojakavyāpāra iti bhāvanocyate | ato yajetetyevamādibhyo’nyotpādanā’vagamyate | yā ca sā’nyotpādanā sā svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargabhāvanā

’vasīyate | atra codayati—kimidaṃ svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti .

svargakāmaṃ bhāvayediti yuktaṃ vaktum | padārthasya hi padārthāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | svarga

kāmaścātra padārthaḥ na svargaḥ ato’sadevedaṃ pratibhāti | atrocyate | satyaṃ svargakāmaḥ

padārthaḥ
 puruṣapradhānatvādasya śabdasya | sa eva ca puruṣaḥ padārthāntareṇa
 liṅādivācyena
[p. 294]

kāryeṇa niyojyatayā sambadhyate | svargaparastvasau saṃbandhaḥ
 svargabhāvanāparyantaḥ sambandhaḥ

ityaidaṃparyaṃ
 svargasambandhaparyantatvaṃG. paratvaṃ
 atra bhāṣyakāreṇa darśitam |
 nanu kimarthaṃ svargāśrayaṇenānyotpādakatā prapañcitā | yāvatā sākṣādapūrvamevāśritya kimiti na punaḥ prapañcyata ityatrāha

—dhātvarthasya karaṇatvapradarśanārtham
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ—yadi ca svargakāmaśabdaḥ svarga(kāmanā)bhāvanāparyanto bhavati tadaivāpūrvādhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyenāpūrvaṃ kriyāvyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ

prasiddhyati | anyathā kriyaiva kāryaṃ syāt | tataśca dhātvarthasya karmataiva bhavet na karaṇatā,

kriyātirikte kārye tatprati phalaṃ ca prati dhātvarthasya karaṇatā siddhyati | nanu ca karaṇatvapradarśanasyaiva kiṃ phalamityatrāha—vakṣyati hi sthita etasminnadhikaraṇe guṇavidhirnāmadheyamiti vicāro bhaviṣyatīti |
 etaduktaṃ bhavati | nāmadheyanirākaraṇārthaṃ bhāvārthagrahaṇamiha kṛtam |

nāmadheyatayā siddhiśca śyenādīnāṃ śabdānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇye sati bhavati | sāmānādhikaraṇyasiddhiśca yāgasya karaṇatve sati, śyenādiśabdānāṃ karaṇavibhaktiyogitvāt | tena karaṇasidhyā

vinā bhāvārthagrahaṇameva na śakyate vyākhyātuṃ iti karaṇatvapradarśanārthaṃ svargaṃ pratyutpādakatvaṃ

darśitam | kathaṃ punaḥ svargaṃ bhāvayedityevaṃ paratā
 darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyevamādervākyasya | uttaraṃ—yathaivaṃparatā tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 vṛtyarthañcābhisaṃyogāt

ityatra | tasmāttebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeteti nigamitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | kā punaratra kriyā ?

[p. 295]

uttaraṃ—vidhyarthaḥ
 vidhirūpo yo’rthaḥ | nanu phalasya karaṇaṃ kriyetyuktaṃ
 phalasya ca

yāgaḥ karaṇaṃ, na vidhyarthaḥ
 tasya karaṇatve avākyārthatvaprasaṅgāt | ucyate | yathā vidhyarthaḥ

karaṇaṃ tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 na ca vākyārthatvaṃG. vyākhyārthatvaṃ
, ātmārthameva karaṇatvāvalambanāt |

taduktam—

ātmasidhyanukūlasya niyojyasya prasiddhaye |

kurvatsvargādikamapi pradhānaṃ kāryameva naḥ || iti |

ihāpi ca bhāṣyakāreṇa te ca yāgadānahomasambaddhāḥ svargasya utpattiṃ vadantīti vadatā

vidhyarthasyaiva karaṇatoktā
 | asya bhāṣyasyāyamarthaḥ—te ca bhāvaśabdāḥ yāgadānahomairabhidheyairabhidhāyakatayā’bhisaṃbaddhāḥ santaḥG. tadviṣayīkaraṇaṃ vidhyarthaṃ vadanti
 tadviṣayābhidhānadvāreṇāpūrvārthaṃ vadanti | tatra ca vidhyarthe

svargotpattiśabdaṃ prayuñjānena vidhyarthasya svargotpādane karaṇatoktā | utpadyate yayā sotpattiriti

utpattiśabdaḥ karaṇasādhanaḥ | nanu yāgādeḥ karaṇatvābhyupagamāt vidhyarthasya karaṇatvaṃ

nopapadyate ityatrāha—tatsambandhāddhi yāgādeḥ
 karaṇatā karaṇībhūtāpūrvaniṣpādakatayaiva dhātvarthasya

karaṇatvaṃ nānyatheti na doṣaḥ | kuta iti praśno’yaṃ kathaṃ punarniyogārthasya G. karaṇatā
karaṇabhāvo’vagamyate ityabhiprāyaḥ |

darśayati |

kimanena granthena pradarśitam
 ? uttaraṃ—saivādhikārikī yuktiḥ
 | yā’sāvadhikāralakṣaṇe

iti hi sthāsyāti |

ato yastasya vācakaḥ śabdaḥ tato’pūrvaṃ pratīyata iti | pūrvāparavirodhaparihāro’yam

 vadato virodhaḥ | taṃ pariharati—tenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvetyavirodhaḥ |

 iti

tato’pūrva pratīyata iti tena bhāvaśabdā apūrvasya vācakā iti brūma
 iti bhāṣyaṃ pratīkenānubhāṣya

tātparyamāha—ato yastasya vācaka iti nigamanametat

pūrvasyāmī1

nanu kasmādevaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyāyana ityatrāha bhāṣyakāraḥ na tu kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stī

siddhānte’napekṣatvaṃ vaktumidamadhikaraṇamārabhyataB. ārabdhavyamityarthaḥ
 ityarthaḥ | kathaṃ sāpekṣatā āśaṅkyate ?

uttaraṃ—yathaiva nāmadheyatvaṃ na sambhavati bhāvārthasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvāt tathā guṇavidhānamapi bhāvārthāśravaṇāt |
 bhāvārthāśrito hi guṇavidhiriti | na cātra bhāvārthaḥ śrūyate

yadāśrito’yaṃ guṇavidhiḥ syāt | yadā guṇavidhitvaṃ nāmatvaṃ ca na saṃbhavati tadā gatyantarābhāvāt

prasaktamaprāmāṇyam | tacca śabdasya na svābhāvikaṃ, api tu pauruṣeyatvakṛtam

A. pauruṣeyatvena
 | ataśca

pauruṣeyatvāpatteḥ prasaktā pramāṇāntarāpekṣatā kṛtsnasya vedarāśeḥ | nanvāgneya iti dravyadevatāsaṃyogaśravaṇāt
 tasya ca yajyarthamantareṇānupapatteḥ yajyartho bhāvārthaḥ
 tataśca tatra

nāmatā guṇavidhānaṃ vā saṃbhavatyeva | kathamaprāmāṇyaṃ ? pūrvapakṣyāha—satyaṃ yadi dravyadevatā

sambandhaḥ śrūyate | ākṣeptā āha-nanu cāgneya iti śrūyate |
 pūrvapakṣī vadati—satyaṃ yadyaṣṭākapālaviṣayo na syāt |
 aṣṭākapālasya hi āgneyā śrūyate | na cāsau saṃbhavatītyaśruta

prāya eva dravyadevatāsaṃbandhaḥ | ākṣeptā pṛcchati—yadyaṣṭākapālaviṣaya eva
 dravyadevatāsaṃbandhaḥ1 syāt tataḥ
 kiṃ sambhavatyeva | aṣṭākapālasyāpyāgneyatvamiti bhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣyuttaramāha—idaṃ tato bhavati avihitaivāṣṭākapālatā pratipattavyā |
 uddeśyatvāt |

na cāvihito’ṣṭākapālo’sti | nanu ca yo’ṣṭasu kāpaleṣu śrṛtaḥ so’ṣṭākapālaḥ
 lokika

uddeśyo bhaviṣyati vācaniko vā kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanāt |
 asyārthaḥ—atha

vā laukika uddeśyo mā bhavatu kapāleṣu śrapayatīti vacanāt | kintu vācanika

evāstu | sarvathā tāvadanenaiva vacanenaivāvihitasyāpyāgneyatā vidhātuṃ śakyā |

pūrvapakṣī dūṣayati—vācanikastāvanna saṃbhavati | aṣṭasaṅkhyāśravaṇāt |
 na hi vācanike

aṣṭasaṃkhyāvidhānamantareṇāṣṭākapālatā labhyate | sāpi saṅkhyā vidhīyata iti cet | dūpayati

—na itaretarāśrayaprasasaṅgāt | āgneye saṅkhyā; saṅkhyāyāmāgneyaḥ iti |
 āgneyānuvādenā

’ṣṭasaṅkhyā vidheyā-āgneyo yaḥ soṣṭākapālaḥ kartavya iti | tathā yo’ṣṭākapālaḥ sa āgneya

B. devatāsamyogaḥ

[p. 232]

iti | aṣṭākapālānuvādenāgneyateti | tasmānna guṇavidhiḥ sambhavati | na ca nāma
 saṃbhavatītyuktam |
 tasmādaprāmāṇyam | yadyaprāmāṇyābhiprāyeṇa pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ kathaṃ tarhi nāmeti bhāṣyakāreṇoktam ?
 samādhātā āha—kvo voktam |
 ākṣeptā āha—uktamupanyāse kiṃ

nāmadheyamuta guṇavidhiriti |
 sandehakāla upanyastam | tatra nāmadheyamiti pūrvapakṣavacanavyaktiḥ guṇavidhiriti rāddhāntavacanavyaktiḥ tathā pūrvapakṣaparigrahasamaye guṇavidhipratiṣedhaṃ

ca kurvatā
 pāriśeṣyāt nāmadheyamityuktaṃ bhavati |
 samādhatte—satyamuktaṃ nāmadheyatvaṃ na

tu vidheyasya
 bhāvārthasyedaṃ nāmateti noktamityarthaḥ | kiṃ tarhyuktamityāha—nāmagrahaṇāt

prasiddhārthatā pradarśitā |
 āgneyapadaṃ vidheyārthaṃ na bhavati | kintu prasiddhārthamityuktam

nāmadheyasyāpi prasiddhārthatvāt | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi kathaṃ sāpekṣatoktetyatrāha—prasiddhiśca

pramāṇāntarādṛte na sambhavatīti sāpekṣatvaṃ prayogasya darśitam |
 rāddhāntamāha—syādetadevaṃ yadi saṅkhyāsambandhe devatāsambandhe ca itaretarāśrayatā syāt | B. na cāsāvasti
nanvetadasti |

kathaṃ nāsti ? uttaraṃ—aṣṭākapālasya hi devatāvidhiḥ |
 pūrvapakṣaṃ smārayati—nanu cāsāva
[p. 233]

prasiddha ityuktam | rāddhāntī vadati—kimabhidheyatayāpi na prasiddhaḥ |
 abhidheyatayā

prasiddha ityarthaḥ | abhidhānaprasiddhiṃ cāśritya vidhānamavakalpate | pūrvapakṣī vadati—na
 abhidheyatayā, prasiddhaḥ kintu kāryatayā |
 aṣṭākapālo hi kārya iti kṛtvā’prasiddhatvamucyate |

rāddhāntyāha—iṣṭamevaita
tkāryatayā’prasiddhatvam | yata eva kāryatayā’prasiddhatvaṃ ata eva

vidhīyate |
 upasaharati—tasmādabhidhānāprasiddhirvidhānaṃ vihanti | na punaḥ kāryatayā’prasiddhiḥ | tasmādyajyarthasyeva siddhamaṣṭākapālārthasyāpi vidhānam | 1prasiddhābhidhānagamyatvaṃ ca darśitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa nānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti
 vadatā | aprasiddhārthatve hi na hyanyenāpi śrapitasya grahaṇaṃ prasaktam, yannirākaraṇāya yujyate vaktum | kapāleṣu

śrapayatīti vacanāt nānyena śrapitaṃ gṛhṇātīti | tasmādyajyarthavat taddhitārtho’pyayaṃ

prasiddhaḥ |
 yathā yatra yajeteti sākṣācchravaṇe yajyarthasyābhidhānataḥ prasiddhiḥ tathā taddhitārthasyāpītyarthaḥ | ato na sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ
 guṇaviśiṣṭakarmavidhānenānapekṣatopapatteḥ |

na cātra prāptārthatā agnihotravadaṣṭā
kapālatāyāḥ saṃbhavati
 iti sāpi vidhīyate yathā

agnihotraṃ juhotītyatrāgnirdevatā na vidhīyate agnidevatāyāḥ praptatvāt tatheha na saṃbhavatīti |

C. prasiddhārthābhidhānaṃ cedaṃ

[p. 234]

barhirājyayoḥ |
 kā punaravāntarasaṅgatirityatrāha—prāsaṅkimidamadhikaraṇam |

kaḥ punaratra prasaṅgaḥ ? uttaraṃ—vidheyenāpyarthena nāmārthānvayo dṛṣṭaḥ aṣṭākapālamiti |

ayamapi cājyaśabdaḥ karmanimittaka eva loke dṛṣṭaḥ |
 saṃskāranimitto’yamājyaśabdo loke

dṛśyate | saṃskṛtāvasthāyāmevājyaśabdaprayogāt | tasmādayamapi kāryānvayīA. kāryārthānvayitvamājyaśabdasya

 kāryatayā’nvayī | kāryānvayyājyaśabdāsyārthaḥ | etadeva darśayati—saṃskāreṇājyaṃ kuryāditi |

ājyaṃ vilayanena saṃskuryādityarthaḥ |
 nanu cākarmanimittakopyājyaśabdaḥ kvaciddeśaviśeṣe

dṛṣṭaḥ, ājyaṃ krayyaṃ, barhirādāya gāvo gatā iti |
 tena kathamekāntataḥ saṃskāranimittatā

śakyā’vasātum | pariharati—sārvabhaumo hi prayogaḥ śrauto bhavitumarhati | naikadeśaprabhavaḥ saṃskāranimittakaśca prayogaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ |
 A. ye saṃskṛte śabdaṃ prayuñjate teṣāṃ tāvatsaṃskāranimittatā
ye tāvadasati saṃskāre śabdaṃ na prayuñcate

teṣāṃ sphuṭaiva saṃskāranimittatā | ya’pi jātimātre prayuñcate te saṃskṛte’pi prayuñjate iti

saṃskṛtaviṣayatvaṃ sārvabhaumam | tadeva ca śrautam | ekadeśe yajjātibacanatvaṃ tadgauṇam |

kasmādevaṃ ? uttaraṃ—prasiddhasya
 hi padasya śraute’rthe gauṇatā saṃbhavati
 nāprasiddhasya |

yathaiva hi jvalane prasiddho’gniśabdo māṇavake tatsādṛśyena prayujyamāno gauṇo bhavati | sarveṣāṃ

saṃskāranimittatā prasiddheti jātimātre gauṇaḥ ekadeśabhavaḥ prayogaḥ | yeṣāṃ tu saṃskṛtamātra

eva prayogaḥ teṣāṃ prayogadvayābhāvānmukhyaḥ prayogaḥ | na cāyaṃ sarvatra ghṛtavacanatayā prasiddhaḥ |

aprasiddhasya ca ghṛte’nyatra gauṇatvaṃ nopapadyate |
 ye saṃskāranimittaṃ śabdaṃ manyante te

jātyantarepi śabdaṃ na prayuñjate tatra sa gauṇo na yuktaḥ anyatra mukhyaprayogādarśanāt | karmanimittatve
 yatra punaḥ ekadeśe ghṛte prayujyate
 asaṃskṛte’pi tatra karmanimittake’pi prayogāt

siddho
 gauṇaḥ, anyatra mukhyaprayogasiddheḥ siddho ghṛte gauṇaḥ | tasmāt saṃskāranimittatvādājyādiśabdānāṃ kāryārthataiva yuktā | atrocyate | kāryārthatayā’nvayo na ghaṭate itaretarāśratvaprasaṅgāt | barhiṣaḥ
 sataḥ prokṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇena ca barhiriti |
 yadvai prokṣaṇādinā

saṃskriyate tadbārhirityucyate | barhiruddeśena prokṣaṇādayo vidhīyanta iti na jñāyate kasya

prokṣaṇādayo vidhīyanta iti | yadyevamitaretarāśrayatvaṃ kathaṃ tarhyaṣṭākapāle
 netaretarāśrayaḥ ?

uttaraṃ—na cetaretarāśrayamabhidhānataḥ prasiddherityuktam | śha punaḥ kāryata evābhidhānaṃ,

tenetaretarāśrayatvamiti vaiṣamyam |
 pūrvapakṣī tvāha—yūpe kathaṃ ?
 yūpaṃ takṣatīti śrūyate |

yūpaśabdaśca kāryasaṃskāravacanaḥ tatra vyaktamitaretarāśrayatvam | anyataḥ prasiddhyabhāvāt

agatikā gatiriyam |
 yathā barhirādāvanyataḥ prasiddhirasti jātimātre prayogadarśanāt tathā

yūpe nāsti tena asati gatyantare eṣāgatirāśrīyate yattakṣaṇārhaṃ yattakṣaṇakarmabhūtaṃ tat takṣaṇena yūpaṃ

karotīti | keyamagatikā gatiḥ |
 ekagranthenāha—kiṃ na śāstrādevāvagamyate ?

ayamāśayaḥ—yadi śāstrāt avagamyeta tadā barhirādiṣvapi tathāstviti | uttaraṃ—satyāṃ

prasiddhau na śāstrādavagamyate yatrānyataḥ prasiddhirasti tatra sāmānyena vyavahāro na yuktaḥ |

viśeṣasyaivāvagamāditi tatreyaṃ śāstrādgatirnāvagamyate | yatprokṣaṇārhaṃ prokṣaṇe karmabhūtaṃ tadbārhiḥ

tat prokṣaṇena saṃskriyata iti | aprasiddhe punaḥ padārthe apratihatatvādvidhyarthasya kalpyate |
[p. 237]

anyathā vidhyarthapratighātaprasaṅgāt | yaduktaṃ sārvabhaumaprayogo gauṇo na saṃbhavati

śrautasyāprasiddheriti ekadeśasthastu
 gauṇassaṃbhavatīti atrocyate—nāvaśyaṃ prasiddhaśrautārthameva gauṇaṃ prayuñjate lokikaḥ
 rājaśabdamiva | yathā rājaśabdāmāryā aprasiddhaśrotārthamapi

gauṇaṃ prayuñjate tathā barhirādiśabdamapi | codayati—tatrāpi
 rājaśabde’pi tulyo doṣaḥ |

ekagranthenāha—vācyasādṛśyapratipattyarthaṃ hi gauṇaṃ prayuñjate | atadbuddhyāpi gauṇaḥ

prayujyata iti haṭho’yaṃ;
 vācyārthasādṛśyapratipādanāya hi gauṇaḥ śabdaḥ prayujyate | mukhye

cārthe’vijñāte na tatsādṛśyaṃ jñāyata iti sādṛśyabuddhāvasatyāṃ gauṇasya prayogo C. ’nimittakaḥ
nirhetukaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate—na prayokturvivakṣāmapekṣate śabdārthasaṃbandhaḥ | saṃbandhāpekṣā hi vivakṣā |

asyārthaḥ—yadyapi prayoktā gauṇabuddhyā prayuṅkte prayojyaśca tathaiva pratipadyate tathāpi pārśvastho

bālaḥ mukhyārthe prayogamadṛṣṭvā mukhyatāmevāvagacchati | sa tathā pratītya vyavahāramapi pravartayet |

tena prayoktṛvivakṣāṃ nāpekṣate pārśvasthasya śabdārthasaṃbandhaḥ, kintu yādṛśaṃ mukhyaṃ śabdārthasaṃbandhaṃ

pratipadyate tādṛśameva punarvyavahārakāle’pi pārśvastho vivakṣati | etadeva dṛṣṭāntapurassaraṃ
[p. 238]

darśayati—uktaṃ hi gopotalikādiṣu aśaktijatvāparijñānādanādiśabdamivaC. anādṛgiva
 prayuñjate | eva

matrāpi vivakṣā’parijñānāt kasyacit prayoktuḥ vivakṣāśūnyamarthāntare prayuñcate
 | jātiviṣaye mukhyaṃ śabdārthaṃ jānatā kenacidekadeśajātena gauṇaḥC. gauṇasaṃskāra
 saṃskārayoginyatajjātīye’pi

barhirādiśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | atra mukhyamanyamarthamaviduṣo gauṇārthavivakṣā’sti | evamajānato

mukhyabuddhiḥ pravṛttā | yathā gopotalikādiṣvanāditvabuddhiraśaktijatvamaviduṣaḥ tannibandhanaśca

śabdārthavyavahāraḥ | kathaṃ punaridaṃ vijñāyate
 bhrāntimūlo’yaṃ saṃskṛtaviṣaye mukhyaśabdavyavahāra

iti | uttaraṃ—loke vede ca vyavahāradarśanāt | vede tāvadājyaṃ vidhāya kvacit ghṛtaśabdena stauti
 tatra yadi saṃskāravacanaḥ ājyaśabdaḥ syāt tatra stutau ghṛtaśabdaḥ upalakṣaṇārthaḥ syāt | jātivacanatve tu svārthaḥ, tena jātivacanataiva vedālocanayā’vasīyate |

loke’pyājyaṃ krayyamiti krayyamiti kriyāsādhananirapekṣaḥ prayogaḥ |
 kriyāsādhanaṃ kriyaiva

saṃskārarūpā sādhanaṃ yasya tannirapekṣaḥ prayogo lokepi dṛṣṭaḥ | nanu ca tatrāpyajñātavivakṣātvaṃ

śakyate vaktum |
 A. saṃskṛtasya mukhyārthatvepi tatsaṃbandhādgauṇaḥ
kenacitsaṃskāramātravācitve’pi tadarhatāmātreṇa gauṇo jātau śabdaḥ

prayuktaḥ tatra B. vivakṣāmaparijñāya
vivakṣā’parijñānāt kasyacinmukhyārthabuddhirutpannaivetyatrāpi śakyate vaktuṃ; tena
[p. 239]

kathaṃ niścayaḥ ? pariharati—ukto vetasādiṣu parihāraḥ iti |
 B. laukikavyavahārācca jātisaṃskārarūpavipratipattau
laukikaviṣayavyavahāravipratipattau vaidikavyavahārasāmānyānnirṇaya iti |
 tenātrāpi vaidikavyavahārasāmyāt

jātau mukhyatā | saṃskṛtaviṣaye tu tadīyasaṃsaskārayogādnauṇaḥ prayoga iti | yadyevaṃ tenaiva

tarhyadhikaraṇena gatam | ucyate—jñātamukhyaprayogasyaiva gauṇaḥ prayoga itīmāmāśaṅkāmapanetumidamadhikaraṇamārabdham | udāharaṇaṃ tu barhirādayo vetasādayaścomayatrāpīti | kiṃ punaḥ prāsaṅkisyāsya
 pramāṇalakṣaṇe prayojanaṃ ? uttaraṃ—anadhyavasāya eva

karmaṇyeva viśeṣo darśitaḥ |
 nāvagatau | pariharati—yata eva karmaṇi viśeṣaḥ ata evānadhyavasāyaḥ prayojanaṃ
 tannivṛttaye’nadhyavasāyo’dhikaraṇasya prayojakaḥ | karmaṇi yadi viśeṣo

na syāt tadā anuṣṭhānaviśeṣo na syāt | anuṣṭhānaviśeṣābhāvādanadhyavasāyoA. ’napaneyaḥ
 na doṣaḥ iti

tannivṛttaye’dhikaraṇamārabdhaṃ, niścaye tvadhyavasāyāt sthitameva prayojanam |

prokṣaṇīṣu || kimarthamidaṃ pṛthagadhikaraṇaṃ ? uttaraṃ—sārvabhaumaikadeśabhavayoḥ
 prayogayoḥ smṛtito’pavādaḥ kathyate |
 saṃskāranimittaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ prayogaḥ jātinimittastvekadeśaprabhavaḥ | tayoḥ smṛtimālocyāpavādaḥ kathyate, yaugikaḥ prokṣaṇīśabda iti | pūrvapakṣamāha

 prayogamūlā hi vyākaraṇasmṛtiḥ sā kathaṃ

prayogamapavadatīti bhāvaḥ | rāddhāntamāha—saṃbhavati hi smṛterānuguṇyaṃ prayogasya |
 smṛtyanuguṇo hi prayogo bhavatyeva | kuta ityāha-yasmāt dvayorapyavayavayogaḥ saṃbhavati |
 yo’pi

cet

 yo’sau smṛtāvaviśeṣaḥ sa yadi viśeṣa upasaṃhriyeta prayogabalena tadā na

kaściddoṣaḥ | tasmāt pūrvavadidamapi | tena tathetyuktam |

vaiśvadeve || kathaṃ punaḥ guṇavidhināmadheyatayā’tra saṃśayaḥ ?
 yāvatā bhāvārthasāmānādhikaraṇyānnāmadheyatvamevātra yuktam | uttaraṃ—guṇavidhipakṣa evātra sāmānādhikaraṇyamupapannataraṃ, pratyayārthatvādbhāvārthasya
 na nāmadheyatā yuktā guṇavidhipakṣa eva tāvadyuktaḥ

 vidhyarthatā

na saṃbhavati tenaiva svabhāvenopanyāsaḥ kṛto
 bhagavatā bhāṣyakāreṇa | kena svabhāvenākhyātārthasya vidhyarthatā, kena vā nāmno na G. nivartiteti
saṃbhavatīti
 praśnadvaye yathā saṅkhyamuttaradvayam |

bhāvārthatayā ākhyātārthasya vidhyarthatā, dravyaguṇābhidhānatayā ca nāmno
 na vidhyarthatā |

yathā caitattathopariṣṭādvyākhyāsyāmaḥ vakṣyāmaḥ
 | atra bhāṣyam—kaḥ punarbhāvaḥ ke vā bhāvaśabdā

iti tadvyācaṣṭe—kaḥ punarayamākhyātārthavyatirikto bhāvo nāma ? kaścākhyātaśabdavyatirikto bhāvaśabdo nāma |
 uttarabhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—prativacanantu
 uttaraṃ tu yajeta dadātijuhotītyeṣāṃ
 yo’rthaḥ sa bhāvārthaḥ ete ca bhāvaśabdā iti nanu ca yāgadānahomaśabdā eta

iti parihārabhāṣyam | tadvyācaṣṭe—nanu sa evāyamākhyātārthaḥ ta evāmī ākhyātaśabdāḥ ityākṣepaḥ naitadevaṃ, yāgādiśabdāścaita

 iti parihārabhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—samādhānaṃ

tu nākhyātārthamātraṃ yajeta dadāti juhotītyatra
 gamyate kiṃ tarhi ? arthāntarotpādakatvamapi,
 karturanyo yo’rthaḥ tadutpādakatvamapi gamyate | na kevalaṃ pūrvāparībhāva evāvagamyate yathā bhavatīti |
 pūrvāparībhāvamātraṃ ākhyātamātrārthaḥG. ākhyātārthaḥ
 | na tathā bhāvayedityatraG. pacatītyatra

anyotpādakatvaṃ tu bhāvārthaḥG. bhāvārthatvaṃ
 tacca bhavatītyatra nāsti | kartureva tatrotpattyavagamāt | yadetaduktamasmābhiḥ taduktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇāpi yajyādyarthaścāto’vagamyate bhāvayediti ca
 ityanena

bhāṣyeṇa | nanvanya evārtho’sya bhāṣyasyeti matvā pṛcchati—kimuktamanena
 bhāṣyeṇa | uttaraṃ

—pūrvāparībhāvaścāto’bagamyate anyotpādakatvaṃ ceti
 tatra G. yatra kāryaścāto
yajyādyarthaścāto’vagamyata

ityanena pūrvāparībhūtayāgādyavagatiruktā | bhāvayedi
tyanyotpādakatvāvagatiḥ | punaścāyamartho

bhāṣyakāreṇa prakaṭīkṛtaḥ | tathā yateta yathā yatkiṃñcidbhavatīti |
 anyotpattyanuguṇaḥ puruṣaprayatno’vagamyata ityarthaḥ | tenaite ākhyātaśabdā api bhāvaśabdā ityucyante |
 yataḥ pūrvāparībhūtānyotpādakatvasvabhāvo yo’rthaḥ sa bhāvārthaḥ tadarthāśca śabdāḥ bhāvaśabdāḥ | ato dravyaguṇaśabdagrahaṇamapi |
 nāmaśabde hi kriyamāṇe pratiyogyartho na nirdiṣṭaḥ syāt nāmaśabdānāmapi keṣāṃ

cidbhāvārthatvāt | nanvevamapi yena svabhāvena kriyārthatvaṃG. akiyārthatvaṃ na ni
 tanna nirdiṣṭaṃ syāditi yadi kaścinmanyate
[p. 290]

taṃ pratyāha—dravyaguṇapratyayotpākatvādakriyārthatvaṃ na nāmatvāt |
 nāmnaḥ āgneya ityādeḥ

kriyārthatvadarśanāt | kiṃ tāvatprāptamaviśeṣeṇeti | ko’bhiprāyaḥ
 evaṃ vadato bhāṣyakārasya |

uttaraṃ—dravyaṃ vā bhavatu kriyārthaṃ kriyā vā bhavatu dravyārthā | sarvathā tāvadaparityaktakiyākārakasambandha eva kāryasaṃbandhaḥ |
 yadi kriyāviṣayo niyogaḥ tadā itarattadarthamiti

nyāyāt dravyaṃ kriyārtham | yadā tu dravyaṃ niyogaviṣayaḥ tadā kriyā dravyārthā | pakṣadvayepi

cāsmin kriyādravyārthapakṣe kriyākārakasambandho na virudhyate | tenāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvādanyatarasya

vidhiviṣayatvam | na caivamanadhyavasāyenāprāmāṇyam | anuṣṭhānasyādhyavasitatvāt | ekarūpameva

hi pakṣadvayeṣyanuṣṭhānaṃ anuṣṭhānānadhyavasāyena cāprāmāṇyam | atrocyate | naivaṃ sidhyati

kriyākārakasaṃbandhaḥ | yadā hi kriyā dravyārthā
 tadā dravyaviṣayo niyogaḥ tadā kriyākārakasambandhastyakta eva bhavati | kasmāt ?
 uttaraṃ—dravyaṃ hi tadā karmabhūtaṃ kriyāyāṃ

pradhānabhūtaṃ dravyaṃ, kriyayā īpsyamānatvāt | īpsyamānaśca karmāpadyeta | na ca karma kārakaṃ

kārakasthā hyutpattiḥ karmeti vakṣyāmaḥ
 | kārakaM. kārakasthā
sya yā avasthotpadyate sā karma na kārakāvasthaiva |

yadi karma kārakaṃ na bhavati kathaṃ tarhi karmaṇi kārake dvitīyetyāgamaḥ | tathā karturīpsita-

[p. 291]

tamaṃ karmeti ca kārakādhikāre
 | etadāgamaparyālocanayāpi karmaṇaḥ kārakatvamavagamyate karmaṇi

kārake dvitīyeti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśāt | tathā īpsitatamaṃ yatkārakaṃ tatkarmetyabhidhānāt |

samādhatte—yatra kriyāphalamabhiniṣpadyate
 tat kārakaṃ karmeti sūtrārthaḥ
 | kriyāphalabhāgitvaṃ

kārakasya karmatvaṃ; na punaḥ kriyāniṣpādakatvaṃ kriyāniṣpādakatayā ca kārakatvaṃ nānyathā | kasmādevaṃ

vidhaḥ sūtrārthaḥ ? ityatrāha—anyathāhīpsitatamagrahaṇamanarthakaṃ syāt
 | na hi kriyāniṣpādakaṃ

kriyayā īpsitatamaṃ bhavati | kriyājanyaphalabhāgitvāvasthā ca kriyayā īpsitatamā bhavati |

tasmāt karmaṇi kārake dvitīyetyapi siddham
 | avasthādvayayogitvāt | tasmāt

kriyārthaṃ
 niyogārthaṃ niyogaviṣayasamarpakaṃ yadā’’khyātaṃ
 bhavati tadaiva dravyaguṇayoḥ kārakatvasiddhiḥ kārakatvena ca
 tayoḥ śravaṇaṃ; naitadanugrahāya ākhyātārthasyaiva niyogārthatvaṃ

niścīyate ata āha—ye bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdāstebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeta yajetetyevamādibhyaḥ |

atra sūtrakāreṇa bhāvārthebhyaḥ kiyā pratīyeteti vadatā hetvantarānupādānāt bhāvārthatvameva heturukta

iti matvā pṛcchati—kutaḥ iti |
 tadvyācaṣṭe—kuta iti praśnaḥ G. kimatra
kimanyathā bhāvārthatvaṃ hīyeta

tadapi bhāvārthasyāniyogaviṣayatve bhāvārthatvameva hīyeta tadā syādayaṃ hetuḥ na caitadastīti bhāvaḥ |
[p. 292]

uttaraṃ—bāḍhaṃ hīyate pūrvāparībhāve hi bhāvārthatvaṃ bhavati
 bhāvaśabdasya pūrvāparībhāvavācitvāt | tathāpi kimityāha—sa ca pūrvāparībhāvo’tra dravyaguṇayoḥ tadarthatve sati

bhavati | anyathā hi godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayeditivadāśrayāvagatireva syāt | na

ca godohanasādhyaṃ praṇayanamavasīyate
 | asyārthaḥ-sādhyatve hi bhāvārthatvaṃ bhavati bhāvārthasya

ca niyogaviṣayatve dravyaguṇayośca tadarthatve sādhyatvaṃ bhāvārthasya sampadyate | dravyaguṇavostu

niyogārthatve āśrayamātratayaivāranmātārthavatvamavagataṃR. khyātārthānāmavagatiḥ
 syāt na sādhyatvena godohanena paśukāmasya

praṇayeditivat | nanu ca godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayedityapi pūrvāparībhūtameva praṇayanaṃ

pratīyate | godohanaṃ ca
 karaṇameva | ato’siddho dṛṣṭāntaḥ | pariharati—satyaṃ pratīyate na

punaḥ godohanena praṇayedityevadvākyārthatayā,
 asminvākye godohanasya vākyārthatvāt tadupakārakatvācca praṇayanasya viparītaḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvo’vagamyate | laukikastvayaṃ kriyākārakabhāvaḥ

śabdenopādīyate | tasmādaniyamapakṣe na niyamena kriyākārakabhāvamajahato vidhyarthatvaṃ

vākyasya pratīyate
 | yadā tāvat kriyāniyogārthā tadā pratīyamānakiyākārakabhāvāparityāgaḥ | dravyaguṇayostu kiyārthatvena uktena nyāyena kiyākārakabhāvaparityāga eveti

nāniyamaḥ | api tu bhāvārtha eva niyogārtha iti | ata idamuktaṃ bhāvārthatvādeveti |
[p. 293]

etaduktaṃ bhavati—evameva saṃbandhāvagamāditi |
 bhāvārthasya niyogārthatayā sādhyatvaṃ

dravyaguṇayostu sādhanatvamevetyevamevaG. tyeva
 sambandho’vagamyate, nānyathetyarthaḥ | tadidānīṃR. tamidānīṃ

pūrvāparībhūtabhāvavyatirekiṇamarthaṃ bhāvaśabdavācyaṃ prapañcayati
—yatpratipādanāyākhyātaśabdopādānamakṛtvā bhāvārthagrahaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ taṃ pūrvāparībhūtārthavyatirekiṇaṃ anyotpādakatvamarthaṃ prapañcayati

—yaḥ āhuḥ kimupi bhāvayediti te svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti brūyuriti |

atra cedamabhimataṃ—liṅādiyuktavākyaṃśravaṇasamanantarabhāvinīṃ pravṛttimavagamya pravartakārthābhidhāyitā liṅādīnāmavasīyate | kāryasya ca pravartakatvāvagamāt tadeva liṅādayo’pyavabodhayantīti

niścīyate | kāryaṃ ca kṛtisādhyaṃ kṛtipradhānamucyate | taduktam | kṛtisādhyaṃ pradhānaṃ

yat tatkāryamabhidhīyate
 iti | tasya cābhidhānaṃ kṛtimanabhidhāya nopapadyate iti liṅādayaḥ

kṛtimapyabhidadhati | kṛtireva ca bhavituḥ prayojakavyāpāra iti bhāvanocyate | ato yajetetyevamādibhyo’nyotpādanā’vagamyate | yā ca sā’nyotpādanā sā svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargabhāvanā

’vasīyate | atra codayati—kimidaṃ svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti .

svargakāmaṃ bhāvayediti yuktaṃ vaktum | padārthasya hi padārthāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ | svarga

kāmaścātra padārthaḥ na svargaḥ ato’sadevedaṃ pratibhāti | atrocyate | satyaṃ svargakāmaḥ

padārthaḥ
 puruṣapradhānatvādasya śabdasya | sa eva ca puruṣaḥ padārthāntareṇa
 liṅādivācyena
[p. 294]

kāryeṇa niyojyatayā sambadhyate | svargaparastvasau saṃbandhaḥ
 svargabhāvanāparyantaḥ sambandhaḥ

ityaidaṃparyaṃ
 svargasambandhaparyantatvaṃG. paratvaṃ
 atra bhāṣyakāreṇa darśitam |
 nanu kimarthaṃ svargāśrayaṇenānyotpādakatā prapañcitā | yāvatā sākṣādapūrvamevāśritya kimiti na punaḥ prapañcyata ityatrāha

—dhātvarthasya karaṇatvapradarśanārtham
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ—yadi ca svargakāmaśabdaḥ svarga(kāmanā)bhāvanāparyanto bhavati tadaivāpūrvādhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyenāpūrvaṃ kriyāvyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ

prasiddhyati | anyathā kriyaiva kāryaṃ syāt | tataśca dhātvarthasya karmataiva bhavet na karaṇatā,

kriyātirikte kārye tatprati phalaṃ ca prati dhātvarthasya karaṇatā siddhyati | nanu ca karaṇatvapradarśanasyaiva kiṃ phalamityatrāha—vakṣyati hi sthita etasminnadhikaraṇe guṇavidhirnāmadheyamiti vicāro bhaviṣyatīti |
 etaduktaṃ bhavati | nāmadheyanirākaraṇārthaṃ bhāvārthagrahaṇamiha kṛtam |

nāmadheyatayā siddhiśca śyenādīnāṃ śabdānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇye sati bhavati | sāmānādhikaraṇyasiddhiśca yāgasya karaṇatve sati, śyenādiśabdānāṃ karaṇavibhaktiyogitvāt | tena karaṇasidhyā

vinā bhāvārthagrahaṇameva na śakyate vyākhyātuṃ iti karaṇatvapradarśanārthaṃ svargaṃ pratyutpādakatvaṃ

darśitam | kathaṃ punaḥ svargaṃ bhāvayedityevaṃ paratā
 darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyevamādervākyasya | uttaraṃ—yathaivaṃparatā tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 vṛtyarthañcābhisaṃyogāt

ityatra | tasmāttebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeteti nigamitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | kā punaratra kriyā ?

[p. 295]

uttaraṃ—vidhyarthaḥ
 vidhirūpo yo’rthaḥ | nanu phalasya karaṇaṃ kriyetyuktaṃ
 phalasya ca

yāgaḥ karaṇaṃ, na vidhyarthaḥ
 tasya karaṇatve avākyārthatvaprasaṅgāt | ucyate | yathā vidhyarthaḥ

karaṇaṃ tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 na ca vākyārthatvaṃG. vyākhyārthatvaṃ
, ātmārthameva karaṇatvāvalambanāt |

taduktam—

ātmasidhyanukūlasya niyojyasya prasiddhaye |

kurvatsvargādikamapi pradhānaṃ kāryameva naḥ || iti |

ihāpi ca bhāṣyakāreṇa te ca yāgadānahomasambaddhāḥ svargasya utpattiṃ vadantīti vadatā

vidhyarthasyaiva karaṇatoktā
 | asya bhāṣyasyāyamarthaḥ—te ca bhāvaśabdāḥ yāgadānahomairabhidheyairabhidhāyakatayā’bhisaṃbaddhāḥ santaḥG. tadviṣayīkaraṇaṃ vidhyarthaṃ vadanti
 tadviṣayābhidhānadvāreṇāpūrvārthaṃ vadanti | tatra ca vidhyarthe

svargotpattiśabdaṃ prayuñjānena vidhyarthasya svargotpādane karaṇatoktā | utpadyate yayā sotpattiriti

utpattiśabdaḥ karaṇasādhanaḥ | nanu yāgādeḥ karaṇatvābhyupagamāt vidhyarthasya karaṇatvaṃ

nopapadyate ityatrāha—tatsambandhāddhi yāgādeḥ
 karaṇatā karaṇībhūtāpūrvaniṣpādakatayaiva dhātvarthasya

karaṇatvaṃ nānyatheti na doṣaḥ | kuta iti praśno’yaṃ kathaṃ punarniyogārthasya G. karaṇatā
karaṇabhāvo’vagamyate ityabhiprāyaḥ |
 kāryātmā vidhyarthaḥ phalasiddhau kathaṃ karaṇamityarthaḥ | uttaravādī tu
[p. 296]

eṣahyartho vidhīyate yathā yāgādinā svargakāmaḥ svargaṃ sādhayedityadhikāralakṣaṇasiddhamarthaṃ

darśayati |
 paṣṭhādye hi karmaṇi niyujyamānasya kāminaḥ kāmasiddhau yāgaḥ karaṇamiti kasya

śabdasya vyāpāra iti codayitvā’yamarthaḥ prayatnenoktaḥ | kena sādhayetsvargaṃ yāgādineti |

kimanena granthena pradarśitam
 ? uttaraṃ—saivādhikārikī yuktiḥ
 | yā’sāvadhikāralakṣaṇe

vidhyarthasya karaṇatve yuktiḥ saivoktā’nena granthena | kuta ityāha—svargaṃ sādhayato’dhikāra

iti hi sthāsyāti |
 kāmyasiddhiparyantaḥ kāmino’dhikāraḥ iti vakṣyati | tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ

svargakāmo hi svargaṃ sādhayitumicchati | sa kena sādhayetsvargamityasyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ yajeteti

vidhīyate | yasya ca śabdasyārthena phalaṃ sādhyate tenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā nānyatheti tato’pūrvamavagamyate

ato yastasya vācakaḥ śabdaḥ tato’pūrvaṃ pratīyata iti | pūrvāparavirodhaparihāro’yam

kutaḥ pūrvāparavirodha āśaṅkyate ityatrāha—pūrvaṃ hi niyogātphalamityuktam idānīṃ yāgātphalamiti
 vadato virodhaḥ | taṃ pariharati—tenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvetyavirodhaḥ |
 yāgasyāpi

karaṇatā karaṇībhūtāpūrvasiddhidvāreṇetyānuguṇyānnāsti virodhaḥ | tato’pūrvaṃ gamyata
 iti

sopaskāraṃ bhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—tato’pūrvameva phalasādhanamiti gamyate phalasādhanamityupaskāraḥ
 |
[p. 297]

yasmādapūrvasiddhiddhāreṇa yāgādeḥ karaṇatā ato’pūrvameva paramakāraṇam | ato yastasya vācakaḥ

tato’pūrva pratīyata iti tena bhāvaśabdā apūrvasya vācakā iti brūma
 iti bhāṣyaṃ pratīkenānubhāṣya

tātparyamāha—ato yastasya vācaka iti nigamanametat
 | upasaṃhāro’yamityarthaḥ | akṣarārthamāha’—yasmādapūrvaṃ sādhayan bhāvārthaḥ phalaṃ niṣpādayati nānyathā kṣaṇikatvāt tena

tadviṣayo vidhyartho liṅgādibhiḥ pratyāyyate tasya ca
 tatpratipādyamānasya viṣayādhīnapratipattittvāt bhāvarthānāṃ ca viṣayabodhakatvāt tato’pūrvaṃ pratīyata ityucyate na punara
pūrvasyāmī1

bhāvaśabdā vācakā iti |
 evaṃ ca darśayan bhāṣyakāraḥ tebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeteti sūtraṃ

vyācaṣṭe-yadbhāvārthebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyata ityucyate tanna sākṣādvācakatvābhiprāyeṇa, kintu viṣayābhidhānadvāreṇeti |

nanu kasmādevaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyāyana ityatrāha bhāṣyakāraḥ na tu kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stī
ti | tadidaṃ bhāṣyaṃ kecidayuktaṃ manyante | anenaiva hi bhāṣyeṇa sarvathaiva

apūrvasya vācakaḥ śabdo nāstītyucyate | taccāyuktam | liṅādīnāṃ tadvācakatvāt ityāśaṅkyāha—na tu kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stīti bhāvārthābhiprāyametat |

R. bhāvārtha
/?/ coll out show 1 in para
[p. 298]

bhāvārthān śabdānabhipretya vācakatvaniṣedhaḥ na śabdamātrābhiprāyeṇa | tadāha—na punarliṅādiśabdo’pi niyogaṃ nācaṣṭe
 | nanu yadyapi sūtre tebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyata iti bhāvārthānāmeva

niyogābhidhāyakatā’vasīyate tathāpi pramāṇābhāvānneyamāśaṅkocitā yannirākaraṇāyedamucyate na tu

kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stīti | tadāha—kathaṃ punarbhāvārthe vācakatvāśaṅkā
 ?

uttaraṃ—kevalasyāprayogāt niyogaśabdasya
 liṅādeḥ | yadi bhāvārthavyatirekeṇa kevalena prayuktena liṅādinā vidhyartho’vasīyateG. vidhīyate
 tadā bhāvārthevācakatvāśaṅkā na syāt | na ca

kevalasya prayogo’sti nityaṃ prakṛtiparasyaiva G. niyoga
prayogaviyamāt | tiḍantasyaiva prayogāt

tiṅanta eva prayujyate bhāvārtho’pi na śuddhaḥ | tena yāvatprayogānuvartinyeva vācakateti

bhrāntānāṃ
 yāvadeva prakṛtipratyayātmakaṃ padaṃ prayujyate tadanusāryeva vācakatvaṃ G. tathaiva
tasyaiva

vācakatvamiti bhrāntānāṃ arthāvagamaṃ muktvā
 anyena prakāreṇa prayogeyattā na siddhyati

yadi kevalasyāpi vācakatvaṃ tadā kimiti kevalo’pi na prayujyate | na hyanyonyārthāvagamaṃ

muktvā prayogaparimāṇopāyaḥ yāvatā hyarthaḥ pratyāyyate tāvadeva prayujyata itīdaṃ mataṃ nirākartumavācaka ityuktam

vidhiścānarthakaḥ kvacit ||

rarthavattve saṃbhavati kevalapramāṇataivoktā yenedamucyate vidhiścānarthakaḥ kvaciditi ?

śrayaṇam | na hyarthāntare prayogaśśakyate nirūpayituṃ
 avartamāne vartamānavyapadeśo’yamiti
vidhipakṣe na śakyate nirūpayitum | vidheyaṃ hi śabdato yathābhūtamavagamyate tathābhūtameva
tadāśrayituṃ śakyate | āpekṣikatvañca karaṇasya madīya eva pakṣe
 stutyarthamannakaraṇatva-
saṅkīrtanaṃ ityetasminneva śakyate nirūpayitum |
 hetuvidhipakṣe punarannakriyāntaratā kena
vāryate |
 annakriyāyāṃ yadeva vyavahitaṃ tadeva karaṇatvena gṛhyata ityarthaḥ |

[p. 50]

śūrpaśabdāditi cet |
 asyārthaḥ—śūrpeṇa homaḥ kartavya iti vidhīyate tena hyannaṃ
kriyate iti nirdeśāt yādṛśaṃ śūrpasya karaṇatvaṃ tādṛśamāśrīyata iti kriyā(na)ntaratā nivāryata
iti cet nivṛttāstarhi darvīpitharādayaḥ
 yadi śūrpanirdeśaḥ tvayā’ṅgīkṛtaḥ tadā darvīpitharādīnāṃ
nivṛttaṃ homaṃ prati karaṇatvaṃ, vyāhataśca hetvartho bhavati
 hetunirdeśārthatvena hi hetvartho
gṛhītaḥ yadi darvīpitharādibhirapi homaḥ kriyeta tadā arthavattā hetunirdeśasya | nivṛtteṣu tu
darvīpitharādiṣu kiṃ hetvarthena yata eva yathokto’yamarthaḥ prāmāṇikaḥ ata eva cedamuktam |
yadi heturavatiṣṭheta nirdeśācchūrpa eveti | hetvavyāghāta iti cet |
 asyārthaḥ—sākṣātparaṃ-
parayā vā yasya sādhakatvaṃ tasyāpi karaṇatvam | tena darvīpitharādīnāmapi annakaraṇatvātteṣvapi
hetvavyāghāta iti cet | avyavasthā tarhi |
 sarveṇaiva hi sākṣātparamparayā vā annaṃ kartuṃ
śakyate | tataścānnakaraṇena homaḥ kartavya ityucyamāne yena kenaciddhomaḥ kartavyaḥ ityuktaṃ
bhaveta ityavyavasthā | avyavasthāyāmapi sa eva hetutvavyāghāto nivartyasyābhāvāt anna-
[p. r051]

karaṇena homaḥ kartavyo nānyeneti | yadi vyavasthitaṃ kiñcidannakaraṇaṃ bhavettadā viśeṣavidhānāt
niyamārthatayā vidhānamarthavat bhavet | na ca vyavasthitaṃ kiñcidannakaraṇaṃ; tena nivartyābhāvāda-
narthako’yamupadeśaḥ arthavattvena ca hetupakṣāśrayaṇam | anarthakatve ca hetutvavyāghātaḥ | nanu anna-

kriyāyāṃ kathaṃ sarvasya karaṇatvaṃ, na hi kṛtasya karaṇatvamupapadyate yenaikenāpi kṛte’nyadapi
karaṇaṃ bhavetū |  A. & B. ajñāpyejñāpye hi yāvadanekamapi jñāpakaṃ bhaveta ekenāpi jñāpite aparasyāpi
jñāpakatvāghātādityāśaṅkayāha-kārakasya ca hetornivartyābhāvepi ātmalābho jñāpakasyeva |
asyārthaḥ&mdash;yathā jñāpakahetornivartyābhāvepi ātmalābho bhavati jñāpitasyāpi jñāpanopapatteḥ
tathā kārakasyāpi yadyapyekena kṛte’parasya kārakatvaṃ nopapadyate tathāpyekasmin kārye sākṣātparaṃ-
parayā vā vahūnāmapi kārakatvaṃ ghaṭata eveti tena kārakāntaramanivartyāpi kārakatvamupapadyate
ityannakriyāyāṃ sarvasya  B. karaṇatvaṃkārakatvam |

kecittu nivartyābhāvepyātmalābho bhavati jñāpitasyāpi jñāpanopapattita iti pathanti tatra
pūrvokto vyāghātaśabdo vipariṇāmena yojanīyaḥ kārakasya ca hetornivartyābhāvepi ātmalābho na
vyāhanyeta jñāpakasyevetyarthaḥ | tasmāddhetuvannigadānāmapi pramāṇatayaivāvasthānam |

 asyārthaḥ
&mdash;aṣṭakā kartavyetyevamādivākyaṃ smaraṇatvenābhimataṃ yena smaryate tatsmaraṇam | pramāṇāntara-
pratīto’rtho yena vākyena pratipādyate aṣṭakāḥ kartavyā ityādinā tadityarthaḥ | bhavatvaṣṭakādi-
 A. adds taccakāraṇaṃ yadabhāve’bhāva eva bhāveca bhāva
 B. adds pūrvaṃ
[p. r070]

vākyeṣu smaraṇasaṃmatiḥ tathāpi smaraṇābhimāne ko doṣaḥ |
 uttaramāha pūrvapakṣī&mdash;ayaṃ doṣaḥ
smaraṇādanumāne prāpnoti na kāryataḥ |
 smaraṇāt pauruṣeyādvākyādanumānena bhavitavyaṃ na
kāryataḥ | yadi kāryato’vagatirūpādanumānaṃ syāt tataḥ smṛtivākyasyaiva kāraṇatvamanumīyate |
na caitadarita | bhavatu smaraṇādanumānaṃ tataḥ kiṃ ? uttaramāha pūrvapakṣī&mdash;smaraṇaṃ hi
pramāṇaviṣayaṃ nārthaviṣayaṃ;
 yadidaṃ smaraṇaṃ vākyarūpaṃ tat pramāṇasyānumāpakamiti pramāṇameva
tasya viṣayaḥ nārthaḥ, arthasyānanumeyatvāt | kimiti | uttaraṃ&mdash;tatpurassaratvādartha-
saṃvidaḥ
, yākyaṃ hi pauruṣeyaṃ kāryabhūtaṃ pramāṇamevānumāpayati nārthamiti ‘api ca pauruṣeyā-
dvacanāt’
 ityatroktam | asmābhiśca nītipathe |

punaḥ pūrvapakṣamākṣipati&mdash;yadi smaraṇātpramāṇamanumīyate siṃddha tarhi prāmāṇyaṃ vedasya

smaraṇena pramāṇe’numite pramāṇena ca kāryarūperthe pratipādite yāṃjanāḥ pratinandantītyevamādīnāṃ
kāryārthatvāt | pūrvapakṣyāha&mdash;na
 siddhayati prāmāṇyamiti brūmaḥ, ayathārthatvepi saṃbhavāt |
 
[p. r071]

yadi hi sarva pauruṣeyaṃ vākyaṃ pramāṇapūrvakameva B. pramāṇameva syāt tadā siddhayatyeva A. sidhyet prāmāṇyaṃ bhrāntyādi-
mūlamapi tu bahulaṃ pauruṣeyaṃ vākyamupalabhyate caityavandanādivat tasmānna kāryato’numānamiti

siddham | yuktyantare cāha&mdash;asmākaṃ caitadanumānaṃ
 ye vayaṃ smṛtivākyāt kāryaṃ prati-
padyāmahe teṣāmidaṃ  C. pramāṇānumānaṃpramāṇamanumānam | na cāsmākaṃ smaraṇavyavadhānādidamupapadyate |

yadi hi vedavākyādeva vayaṃ kāryamavagacchema C. avagacchāmaḥ tato’smākaṃ kāryāvagatilakṣaṇaṃ vāryamupapadyate |
smaraṇavyavadhāne tu tannopapadyate | kasmādityāha&mdash;na kāryaṃ smaraṇasaṃbandhi kāraṇasaṃbandhiṃ tat |

yatkāryāvagatilakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na tat pauruṣeyavākyasaṃbandhi | api tu smaraṇakāraṇabhūtavedavākya-
saṃbandhi tat | na ca vyavahitasya kāryasya nirapekṣaṃ tadeva
 mūlabhūtaṃ vedavākyamastītyanapekṣaṃ

kāryāvagatilakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ; tasmāt vandhyāsutasmaraṇatulyamidaṃ
 kāryāvagatilakṣaṇaṃ kāryama |

yathā bandhyāsutasmaraṇamasiddhaṃ tathā kāryāvagatirapyasiddheti hetvasiddhayā nānumānodayaḥ |
nanu cānidaṃprathamatāpi tathātve hetuḥ dṛṣṭā yathā śabdārthāvagacau vedo’yaṃ vrāhmaṇo’yamiti |

asyārthaḥ&mdash;yadā vedaśabdavācyo’yaṃ granthaḥ brāhmaṇaśabdavācyo’yaṃ puruṣa iti śabdārthasaṃbandhāvagatau
puruṣavacasāṃ  vyavadhānāreva tuyathārthatve’nidaṃprathamatā hetuḥ | ekasmāt puruṣavacanādupalabhya aparaṃ puruṣavacanaṃ
prayuktaṃ; tadapyaparasmāditi tathehāpyekasmaraṇādaparaṃsmaraṇaṃ tadapyaparasmāditi | pariharati&mdash;
viṣama upanyāsaḥ, dṛṣṭārthe vacane’nidaṃprathamatāyuktā | arthadarśanameva hi tatrara mūlatāṃ
 A. yathārthahetutve

pratipadyate |
 asyārthaḥ&mdash;tatra sarveṃṣāṃ vyavahārāvagatilakṣaṇakāryamūlaṃ śabdārthasaṃbandhadarśanameva
vākyaprayogamūlam | iha tu

 agnihotraṃ juhotītyevamādibhyaḥ | na ca viparyayo’sti

[p. r073]

nanu ca kāraṇābhāvāt duṣṭakāraṇatvaṃ;
 yadā hi uktena nyāyena kāryapratipattau śavdasya kāraṇatvaṃ
nirastam tadā kāraṇābhāvāt duṣṭakāraṇatvamevāvagamyate | rāddhāntī pṛcchati&mdash; kimabhāvaḥ
kāraṇe śakyate vaktuṃ;
 na śakyata iti bhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣī pṛcchati&mdash;kimiti na śakyate |

rāddhāntī tūttaramāha&mdash;vedasaṃyogasya saṃbhavāt |
 asyārtaḥ&mdash;aṣṭakāḥ kartavyā ityevamādi-
vākyapraṇetdṛṇāṃ yadā vedasaṃyogaḥ saṃbhavati tadā tata eva nirapekṣāt kāryāvagamāt ‘yāṃ janāḥ
pratinandanti’
 ityevamādīnāmapi siddhaṃ svābhāvikaṃ kāryārthatvam | kāryārthatve siddhe siddhaṃ
svābhāvikaṃ sarvaśabdānāṃ kāryārthatvamiti na kāraṇābhāva. śakyate vaktum | ataḥ kāraṇa-
doṣo’pi na śakyate vaktum | pūrvapakṣyāha&mdash;satyaṃ kāraṇasyābhāvo na śakyate vaktuṃ
bhāvo’pi naiva; bhrāntyādimūlasyāpi saṃbhavāt |
 rāddhāntī pariharati&mdash;nanu kāryata
eva bhāvo’vagamyate kāraṇasya;
 kāryatāvagatilakṣaṇāt kāryāt kāryāvagatau kāraṇasya
bhāvo’vagamyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati-aṣṭakāḥ kartavyā ityasyāḥ kāṃryāvagateḥ kāraṇaṃ parikalpyata
iti | pūrvapakṣyāha&mdash;na
 kāraṇasya bhāvo’vagamyate smaraṇavyavadhānādityuktam | kimidaṃ
smaraṇavyavadhānāditi ?
 uttaram&mdash;idaṃ tat
 smaraṇavyavadhānāditi smaraṇaṃ hyatra pramāṇa-

[p. r074]

tayopalabhyate A. & B. palakṣyate |
 adhunātanapramātdṛṇāṃ smṛtivākyameva kāryāvagatau kāraṇam | na tu
vedavacanam | rāddhāntī pṛcchati&mdash;tataḥ kiṃ ? yadi smaraṇādeva kāryāvagatiḥ |
 pūrvapakṣī
tūttaramāha-idaṃ tataḥ tayā tadgatameva prāmāṇyamanumātuṃ śakyate, na śabdagataṃ, upalabdhasya hi
kāraṇasya  C śakyamanumānaṃśaktyanumānaṃ kāryataḥ nānupalabdhasya |
 yato hi kāryāvagatiḥ tasyaiba śakti-
ranumātuṃ yuktā | smaraṇācceyaṃ kāryāvagatiḥ na vedāditi smaraṇasyaiva prāmāṇyamanumātuṃ yuktaṃ na
vedasya |

nanu ca smaraṇameva nopapadyata iti kāraṇāntarānumānaṃ vedānumānam |
 pūrvapakṣī
pṛcchati-kasya kāraṇāntarānumānam ? ki smaraṇasya, āhosvit kāryāvagatilakṣaṇasya
kāryasya | uttaram&mdash;kāryāvagatilakṣaṇasya kāryasya
 kāraṇāntarānumānam | pūrvapakṣyāha&mdash;
maivaṃ voco rāddhāntin |
 na smaraṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāryāvagaterityuktam |
 tena tatra kāraṇānta-
rānumānamayuktam | rāddhāntī vadati&mdash;smaraṇasya
 manvādivākyasya tarhi kāraṇāntarānumānam |
pūrvapakṣī vadati&mdash;yadi smaraṇasya kāraṇāntarānumānaṃ kimāyātaṃ kāryasya ?
 kāryāvagatilakṣa-
ṇasya  omits nana tadīyaṃ tāvat kāraṇamanumitamityarthaḥ | kuta ityāha&mdash;na hyanyakāraṇaṃ anyasya

[p. r075]

kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | tena smaraṇasya kāraṇaṃ na kāryāvagateḥ kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | ataḥ
smaraṇakāraṇepyanumite na kāryāvagateḥ kāraṇamanumitaṃ bhavati | nanu ca smaraṇakāraṇamapi
praṇāḍyā
 kāryāvagateḥ kāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyati
 tena kāryāvagatiḥ smaraṇakāraṇamanumāpayiṣyati |
pūrvapakṣī pariharati&mdash;yadyanyakāraṇamapi praṇāḍhyā anyasya kāraṇaṃ bhavati kāraṇāttarhi kārya
vijñeyam | na kāryataḥ kāraṇam |
 asyārthaḥ&mdash;prathamaṃ kāraṇamavagamya asyedaṃ kāryamiti jñāna-
māpadyate na punaḥ kāryabalenaiva kāraṇatvaparikalpanam | kasmādityāha&mdash;kāryaṃ hi kāraṇamātra-
paryavasāyi
 na  C. omits tattatpraṇāḍīmapekṣate | kāryāddhi kāraṇe parikalpyamāne yadeva tasya sākṣāt
kāraṇaṃ tasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ parikalpyate | na punaḥ kāraṇakāraṇasyāpi | kuta ityata āha&mdash;
kāraṇaniṣpattirhi  kāryodbhūtaukāryotpattau hetuḥ na tanniṣpādakamapi |
 tena yadeva kāryotpattau
hetuḥ  C. omits thistadevāpekṣate yadanantarameva kāryamutpadyate tasyaiva kāryatvabalena kāraṇatvaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyate
na kāraṇakāraṇasyāpi | yastu kāraṇakāraṇasyāpi kāraṇatvamicchati tena balādidamabhyupaga-
manīyam | vyavahitasyāpi pramāṇāntareṇa kāraṇatvamavagamya tasyedaṃ kāryamityavagamyata iti |
tacca pramāṇāntaraṃ nāsti | tasmānna kāraṇakāraṇaṃ kāryasya kāraṇam | ato’saṃbaddhaṃ tat | kāryeṇā-
saṃbaddhasya cānumāne’navasthā prāpnoti |
 atra codayati-nanu ca niyatakāraṇakāraṇakaṃ hi
 C. tasyedaṃ

yatkāryaṃ tat kāraṇakāraṇamapyavagamayati A. anumāpayati | |
 tena asaṃbaddhasyāpi kāraṇakāraṇasya
kāryato’numānamasti yathā paṭādibhyaḥ kuvindādeḥ | paṭasya hi sākṣāt tantavaḥ samavāyi-
kāraṇam tatsaṃyogastvasamavāyikāraṇam | kuvindādayastu asamavāyikāraṇabhūtatantusaṃyoga-
syotpattāvapi na kāraṇaṃ sākṣāt | tathā hi&mdash;tantukarmabhyaḥ tantusayogāḥ | tantukarmāṇi ca
kuvindahastatantusaṃyogebhyaḥ | paṭastu tantusaṃyogebhya iti | atha ca paṭādibhyaḥ kuvindā-
dayo’pyanumīyanta iti | pūrvapakṣī pariharati&mdash;maivaṃ maṃsthāḥ asaṃbaddhaṃ
 kāraṇakāraṇamapyanumīyata
iti | etāvadeva hi tatra kāraṇam |
 tantutatsaṃyogakuvindādikameva paṭotpattau kāraṇam |
nimittakāraṇasyāpi kāraṇatvāt kuvindādayaśca paṭotpattau nimittakāraṇam tena nāsaṃbaddhatvamiti
yuktameva teṣāmanumeyatvaṃ ca | pūrvapakṣī rāddhāntino matamāśaṅkate&mdash;kimevaṃ smṛteḥ śabdakāraṇa-
kāraṇataivāsmin kārye |
 asyārthaḥ&mdash;yathā paṭādau janayitavye tantusaṃyogānāṃ kuvindakāraṇa-
karmakāraṇatā evaṃ smṛterapyasmin kāryāvagatilakṣaṇakārye janayitavye śabdakāraṇakaṃ yadvijñānaṃ
tatkāraṇataiveti kiṃ siddhāntin manyase yena śabdamanumāpayi C. anumānumitumicchasi | siddhāntī tūttara-
māha&mdash;omityucyate |
 evamevāṅgīkaromītyarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣī dūṣayati&mdash;siddhaṃ tarhi caitya-
vandanādīnāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ
, teṣāmapi śabdakāraṇakāraṇatvāt śabdānumāne sthite tanmūlatvena
prāmāṇyopapatteḥ |

[p. r077]

pūrvapakṣī rāddhāntino matamāśaṅkate&mdash;na
 siddhayati caityavandanādīnāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ, śabdamūlatvena
praṇetṛbhiranabhyupagamāt | tathā hyāha śauddhodani A. buddhamuniḥ:&mdash;“paśyāmyahaṃ bhikṣavo divyena cakṣuṣā sugati
durgatiṃ ca”
 iti | na ca divyacakṣurupapadyate yadyogajaṃ sarvaviṣayajñānamabhimatam | na ca
tathāvidhasya jñānasya kāraṇamasti | ato nirmūlatvāt caityavandanādismṛtīnāṃ aprāmāṇyamiti
cet | dūṣayati&mdash;na tarhi kāryato viśiṣṭakāraṇānumānam |
 yadi hi caityavandanādismṛtiṣu
śabdakāraṇakāraṇatā A. & C. śabdakāraṇatā vyabhicaritā tadā smṛtiḥ vyamicāriṇī satī na śabdamanumāpayitumalam |
tasmāt&mdash;pramāṇāntaramatra mṛgyam |
 mūlabhūtaśabdasadbhāvāvadhāraṇe na ca tadastītyuktam |

yatra na vaidikaṃ śabdamūlamupalabhata iti pramāṇāntareṇa vaidikaśabdopalaṃbhābhāva evedamadhi-
karaṇam | pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati&mdash;tadidaṃ vandhyāsutasmaraṇavadakāraṇaṃ
, yathā’tyantānupalabdha-
vandhyāsutādismaraṇaṃ na pramāṇasadbhāve kāraṇaṃ tathedamapyaṣṭakādismaraṇam | nanu mā bhūta smaraṇaṃ
pramāṇasadbhāve kāraṇaṃ, svayameva tu svatantramarthaparicchedakāraṇaṃ bhavatu ityatrāha&mdash;na  A. na tu smatat smaraṇa-
tayā karaṇībhavitumarhati
 na svatantraṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | smaraṇatvādevetyarthaḥ |

tasmāt duṣṭakāraṇataivātra sthitā | tataścāprāmāṇyaṃ codanāyāḥ |
 rāddhāntamāha&mdash;aho’na-
vasitanayanītikṣo bhavān |
 na bhavatā nayo’vasita.; nāpi nītijñāḥ mīmāṃsakāḥ mīmāṃsakokto
nayaḥ, mīmāṃsakāśca nītijñāḥ | tena mīmāṃsakoktaṃ nayaṃ mīmāṃsakāṃśca nītijñān anādriyamāṇaḥ bhavāna
anavasitanayanītijñaḥ | pūrvapakṣī pṛcchati&mdash;kimiti |
 rāddhāntī tṛttaramāha&mdash;kāryatastāvat

yathā ca pratyakṣamūlāni laukikāni vacāṃsi vaktrabhiprāyāntarbhāvadvāreṇa arthapratītimupajanayanti
sadva B. tathādaṣṭakādivākyānyapi | tena kāryāvagatirevāsiddheti na śakyate vaktum | tena

saṃbhavādvedasaṃyogasya kāraṇāntarasyāsaṃbhavādasmin kārye |
 asyārthaḥ&mdash;evaṃ smaraṇasya
kāryāvagatau B. smaraṇakāryāvagatau kāraṇatve pratibhāsamānepyakāraṇatvaṃ bhavet yadi smaraṇameva pramāṇalakṣaṇakāraṇaśūnyaṃ

[p. r079]

bhavet | na ca smaraṇasyaivaṃvidhasya kāraṇaṃ nāsti smaraṇādeva kāryabhūtāt kāraṇānumānāt |
kāraṇe cānumīyamāne kāryāvagatiśaktasmaraṇalakṣaṇakāryānuguṇatvena veda evānumīyate | pramāṇā-
ntarasyānanuguṇatvāt | na hi pramāṇāntarāṇi evaṃvidhasmaraṇakāraṇatāmāsādayitumarhanti
kāryaṃ prati tasya pramāṇatvābhāvāt | na ca vedānumānamapyasaṃbhavānnirastam; vedasaṃyogasya
saṃmavātsmṛtikārāṇāmapi | nanu na saṃbhavati smṛtikārāṇāmapi vedasaṃyogaḥ vedasyānupalabdheḥ |
yatraiva hi viṣaye sarveṣāṃ vedānupalaṃbhaḥ tadviṣayasmṛtigocara evāyaṃ vicāraḥ | anyatra vicārasya
niravakāśatvāt | na ca tatra granthastvanumīyata iti granthānumānena rāddhāntopavarṇanaṃ yujyate |
 A. nahyapratyana hi pratyakṣasya granthasyānumānaṃ yuktam | na cedaṃ vātyaṃ, yadyapi smṛtikārāṇāṃ pratyakṣaveda-
saṃyogo nāsti tathāpyanumānanibandhano vedasaṃyogo bhaviṣyatīti | liṅgābhāvāt | anumitaśca kathaṃ
vedo bhaviṣyati iti, kathaṃ ca tasyārthabodhakatvam |

atrocyate&mdash;anumito’pi tāvacchabdo bodhaka B. & C. vācaka eva eva, avagatasya sataḥ smaraṇārūḍhasya
vācakatvāt | ata eva lipyavagatebhyo’kṣarebhyo’rtha  C. kṣarapratipattiḥpratipattiḥ | na cānumeyasyāvedatvam,
vedo hi nāma apauruṣeyaḥ śabda C. sandarbhaḥsaṅghātaḥ | na cānumīyamānasya tadānīṃ aśrotragrahaṇatayā
śabdatvamevānupapannam | lipyavagatasyeva yogyatayā hi śrotragrahaṇatā tasya vidyate tasyāpi
kakārādivarṇamayatvāt | yacca liṅgābhāvādananumānamupanyastaṃ tadudāharaṇamātraniṣṭhatayā, yathaiva
hyasmākaṃ smṛtivākye darśanasamutthamanumānaṃ tathā manvādīnāmapi smṛtyantaradarśanasamutthitamityanena
krameṇāpareṣāmaṣyanumānamapareṣāmapītyanādismṛtiparaṃparāśrayaṇānna kaściddoṣaḥ | anāditvepi ca
smṛtiparaṃparāyāḥ  A. smṛtismṛtereva draḍhimā pramāṇaṃ;  C. tenātrāpyatenānādāvapyandhaparamparādidoṣo’pi nāsti | sarveṣāṃ
smṛtilakṣaṇaliṅgamayalocanaśālitvāt | śabdārthasaṃbandhavat | yathā hi śabdārthasaṃbandhāvagame
pūrvapūrvavṛddhavyavahāraliṅga liṅgasaṃbandhasaṃbhavātsadbhāvāt nāndhaparaṃparādoṣaḥ tathehāpi | na ca granthānumānābhyupagame’pa-
siddhāntadoṣaḥ | artho hyarthāpattau na śabdamupasthāpayati | tasyākāraṇatvāt | smṛtistu

ityāha | anyathā pratyakṣavedamūlatve sarbasmṛtīnāṃ itarepyāśramāḥ pratyakṣavedavihitā eveti

bhavatītyatrāha&mdash;caityavandanādiṣvasaṃbhavāt |
 labdhātmakaḥ kāraṇābhāvaḥ | asyārthaḥ&mdash;grantha-
praṇetṛbhiḥ caityavandanādiṣu yatkāraṇamabhyupagataṃ  C. omits tanna saṃbhavatitanna saṃbhavati yogipratyakṣādi tasya tena teṣāṃ
vandhyāsutasmaraṇavadaprāmāṇyam | manvādayastu vedamūlatvameva svavacasāṃ pratijānate |
yaḥ kaścitkasyaciddharmo manunā parikīrtitaḥ |

sa sarvo’bhihito vede sarvajñānamayo hi saḥ ||

iti bruvāṇāḥ | tasmādvaiṣamyaṃ caityavandanādibhiḥ aṣṭakādīnām | ato bhavadīyamevātra
kāraṇadoṣānumānaṃ nirastaṃ, na punarasmadīyaṃ kāryato granthānumānam | tasmādatantramanupalabdhiḥ,
granthaviṣayā
, na nirmūlatāṃ smṛterāpādayitumalamityarthaḥ |

yadyapi hi kasyāṃcicchākhāyāmanāmnātatvāt | anabhivyaktatayā grantho nopalabhyate tathāpi
tasyānumānamaviruddhaṃ, anabhivyaktatayā yadapi vākyaṃ nopalabhyata iti | etadevopapāda yati
vismaraṇopapatteśca pratipattaḥ |
 yadi hyanāmnāto’pi śabda upalabhyate tataḥ pratipattuḥ
vismaraṇaṃ nopapadyate | dṛśyate ca pratipattdṛṇāṃ vismaraṇamapi | tato’vagacchāmo’nāmnātasya
satopyanupalaṃbhanaṃ bhavatīti | tasmādavyāhatamanapekṣatvaṃ codanāyāḥ |
 yatpunaridamuktaṃ ‘yāṃ

bhedaḥ|
 yasya hi kratau dṛṣṭaṃ kārya dakṣiṇādānasyeva tasya tāvat kratunā’’kṣiptatbāt kevalaṃ
niyamamātra eva vacanasya vyāpāraḥ | na ca niyame lobhasya mūlatā saṃbhavati niyamasya puruṣaṃ pratyanuṣayo-
gāt | ata eva ca suvarṇaśrṛṅgīdānādismṛtirapi na lobhamūlā | paropakāraḥ kartavya
iti pratyakṣeṇa vedenaiva paropakāre vihite tadbalenaiva dhanadānākṣepe niyamamātre vacanasya vyāpāraḥ |
tatra ca na lobhaḥ saṃbhavati iti na lobhamūlāsau | yasya punaḥ kratau dṛṣṭaṃ nāsti tasya dānameva
vacanagamyam | tatra lobhasya saṃbhavāt tanmūlatvameva | tasmāt kratāvadṛṣṭārtham dṛṣṭakāraṇaṃ

saṃbhāvitalobhādimūlamapramāṇamiti yuktamapramāṇābhidhānam |
 ata eva cādṛṣṭārthaṃ kratāva-
pramāṇam&mdash;taduktaṃ vaisarjanahomīyasya vāsaso dṛṣṭārthaṃ dānamityaprāmāṇyam |

śiṣṭākope’viruddhamiti cet |
 śrutyā saha smṛtervirodhe cāvirodhe cāprāmāṇyapari-
hāro ya uktaḥ tena sthitaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ codanāyāḥ | sa evāyaṃ virodhaḥ kvāsti kva nāstīti

[p. r093]

cintyate hānopādānārtham |
 yadi virodho’sti tadā hānaṃ smṛteḥ kartavyam | atha nāsti
tadopādānam | atra codayati&mdash;viṣayavyāptipradarśanaṃ kimartham ? nyāyasvarūpābhidhāna-
paraṃ hyetacchāstram, na lakṣyaniṣṭham |
 nyāyasvarūpābhidhānamanena śāstreṇa kriyate | tena
virodhe hānavirodhe vā upādānaṃ smṛterityukte kimarthaṃ virodha evāsti nāstīti cintyate |
pariharati&mdash;satyaṃ yathā bhavānāha nyāyasvarūpābhidhānamevedaṃ viṣayavyāptyā pradarśyate | sva-
rūpe’bhihite’pi nyāyasya kasmiṃścidviṣaye’sadiva pratibhāti kāryadhiyām | atastadapanuttaye
kṛtadhībhirayaṃ yatnaḥ kṛtaḥ | yathā’sminnevodāharaṇe kramakālavirodhaṃ manvānasya

śāstraparicchinnā’ṅgānām | smṛtyunusāreṇa dakṣiṇenaikena hastenānuṣṭhāne A.& B. addvikalpyate viruddhayate vilaṃbāpatteḥ |

kathamiti |

nānuṣṭhānamiti pañcame sthitam | teṣāṃ pratyakṣaśrutatvāt | ānumānikāstu smārtāḥ paścātkrama-

sati tadapakarṣe virodhaḥ |

tannirākuryāt tadā syādaprāmāṇyam ? atha tu yatraiva
 yavādiviṣaye pratyarthī
 vedavākyaśeṣo
vidyate tatraiva kāryānumānavirodhaḥ tadā vihatapratipakṣaṃ
 kāryānumānaṃ kena vāryate |
 yasya
kāryānumānasya pratipakṣo vihataḥ tasya kena vāraṇaṃ śakyate kartum ? nanu sāmānyatodṛṣṭenaivāsya
nivāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyati |  A. tadāpitadāha&mdash;kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ na bhavatīti |

uttaram&mdash;sāmānyameva hi tanna syāt apratipakṣasiddhau |
  A. satipratipakṣe anārthasapratipakṣānāryavyavahāratvama-
nimittatvenaiva vyāptaṃ, na kevalamanāryavyavahāratvaṃ, tena sāmānyamevāpratipakṣasya pikādi-
viṣayasyānāryavyavahārasya yavādiviṣayeṇānāryavyavahāreṇa saha nāsti | tasmādapratipakṣamleccha-
vyavahārasiddhamapi nimittameva codanārthāvagatau |
 ato mlecchāryavyavahārayoḥ virodhe
pūrvādhikaraṇe, avirodhe ca atra nirastaḥ pratipakṣaḥ sāpekṣatvalakṣaṇo yasya tadanapekṣatvaṃ
siddhaṃ codanāyāḥ |

[p. r107]

prayogaśāstramiti cet | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ pauruṣeyatayā siddhasya
 prayogaśāstra-
syānapekṣatvamāśaṅkayate |
 māśakādīni pauruṣeyatvenaiva smaryante | yacca puruṣavākyaṃ
tatsāpekṣaṃ, ataḥ kathaṃ teṣāmanapekṣatvamāśaṅkayate | uttaram&mdash;kāryataḥ pramāṇataścāśaṅkayate |

etadeva vivṛṇoti&mdash;kāryaṃ paṭhitasya pāthaḥ śākhāntaravat | pramāṇaṃ punaḥ ācāryavacaḥ
pramāṇamiti |
 asyārthaḥ&mdash;yadidaṃ kāryaṃ paṭhitasyaiva vākyasya punaḥ pāthaḥ yathā
śākhāntare tadālocanayā anapekṣatvamevāvagamyate&mdash;vedavākyamevedaṃ maśakādibhiḥ paṭhyate
tasyānapekṣatvaṃ yuktameva | na hi puruṣeṇa paṭhitamityetāvatā sāpekṣaṃ vedavāk‏yameva bhavati,
ācāryavacaḥ pramāṇamityanenāpyanapekṣatvameva gamyate |  A. na hi pramāṇasya sāpekṣatvamastina
 hi sāpekṣasya prāmāṇyamasti |
nanu ca vipratiṣiddhamidaṃ pauruṣeyamanapekṣaṃ ceti |
 pariharati&mdash;kimatra vipratiṣiddham ?
yathāvagamyante’rthāḥ tathā bhavanti | dvayaṃ cātrāvagamyate pauruṣeyatvamanapekṣatvaṃ ceti |
smaraṇāt pauruṣeyatvaṃ
 tenaiva tadupapadyate kāryavacanābhyāmanapekṣatvam | tasmād‏dvayamastu |

 A. sāpekṣatvaṃ

atredamabhipretaṃ&mdash;ekaikaṃ vāk‏yaṃ vaidikameva tenānapekṣatvaṃ tāni tu nānāśākhāprakaraṇānārabhya-

yadīha anuṣṭhātṛmātravartī itarapuruṣavyāvṛtto guṇo jātirvā śarīrasanniveśo vā bhavet tato bhavenni-
yāmakaḥ | na cāsāvasti | tathā samākhyāpi
 dākṣiṇātyādikā na niyāmikā |
nanu yo yatra jātaḥ sa tena deśaviśeṣeṇa samākhyāyate prācya udīcyo dākṣiṇātya iti

tena samākhyā niyāmikā bhavatu | pariharati&mdash;na janmamātrāt samākhyeyamāgatādiṣvapi

[p. r114]

prāk‏kvacit jātaḥ paitṛkamevācāramanuvartate na prācyaṃ; tathā itaro’pi itaratra jāta iti |
lohitākṣādayastu niyāmakatvena vyabhicaritāḥ |
 tasmāt siddhamavyavasthitatvam |

kiṃ punarasyāścintāyāḥ pramāṇalakṣaṇaupayikatvamasti ?
 yeyaṃ holākādayaḥ padārthāḥ
prācyādibhireva kartavyāḥ uta sarvairiti asyāścintāyāḥ kiṃ pramāṇalakṣaṇaṃ pratyupāyatāsti yenātreya-
mārabdhā yāvatā tannāstyeva | uttaram&mdash;kathaṃ vā nāsti
 api tvastyeva | ākṣeptā
āha&mdash;yadi niyāmakamasti yadi vā nāsti kiṃ jātamanapekṣatvasya |
 samādhātā āha
&mdash;idameva vakṣyamāṇaṃ jātam | abhimāno vidyate na ca tasya mūlamupalabhyata ityaprāmāṇyaṃ
pūrvapakṣī manyate |

na saṃbhavatīti kevalaholākādipratipādikā codanā ayathābhūtārthatayā na pramāṇam codanā tathā-

utānyenāpi iti | sā viṣayaśuddhayarthatvena draṣṭavyā |  A. teṣāmevāvyavasthitamitisarveṣāmanuṣṭheyamiti viṣayaṃ śodhayitvā

 tarhi vedasyeti | rāddhānte tu nābhimānaprabhavaṃ

 pratipādayitavyaḥ iti syāt | tadā bhavedaprāmāṇyaṃ

codanāyāḥ | na caitadasti svātantryāccodanāyā | abhimānasya ca mūlaṃ nāstīti |
bhavatvasau ayathārtho’bhimānaḥ |

 sarvaśabdānā-
