nāsti; asti tvasau | dṛśyate hi bālānāmanyaśabdoccāraṇecchāyāmapi śabdāntaroccāraṇaṃ

yathā kartecchāyāṃ katteti viṣṇābucciccārayiṣite viṇṇuriti ca | idamatrābhipretaṃ&mdash;kenacit
gośabdamuccārayitukāmena apaṭukaraṇena A. kāraṇadoṣeṇa pramādādinā vā gāvītyuccāritam | tatra vṛddhāntareṇa
kenacit prakaraṇādinā tadīyāṃ vivakṣāmavagamya gośabdādarthaṃ pratītya tena vyavahāraḥ pravartitaḥ |
tatrānyābhyāmavyutpannābhyāṃ bhrāntyaivamavadhāritam | gāvīśabdādevānenāyamartho’vyavadhānena prati-
panna iti | tataśca tābhyāṃ sāsnādimati vācakatbaṃ gāvīśabdasya bhrāntyaivāvasitam | tathā’va-
sāyinau tena śabdena vyavahṛtavantau tadvayavahārāt bālāḥ apare’vyutpattyā vyavaharantītyādimattayā
vyavahārasiddhiḥ | punaḥ pūrvapakṣyāha&mdash;satyaṃ vālānāmanyaśabdoccāraṇamupalabhyate |
 tacca
tadvayavahārāvadhikameva | tasya bālasya vyavahārādārabhya pravṛttamiti tat smaryate | yatpunaḥ

[p. r144]

asmaryamāṇaprabhavaṃ tadapyaśaktijamityaśaktijamevābhidhānam |
 tena gāvyādīnāmaśa-
ktijatvaṃ na śakyate vaktum | tadidaṃ sāmānyato dṛṣṭenocyate-gāvyādayaḥ śabdāḥ anya-
śabdoccāraṇecchāyāmaśaktena kena ciduccāritāḥ vyavahārāṅgatvāt  A. tarkakarkaśabdavaditi | na cedaṃ
sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ ghaṭate; smaryamāṇaprabhavasya hi vyavahārāṅgatā aśaktijatvena vyāptā, nāsmaryamāṇa-
prabhavasya | tena sāmānyameva nāsti | tathā’naikāntikatā ca tāvat gavādiśabdaiḥ | tatheṣṭa-
vighātakāritā, lakṣaṇāviruddhatā ca; gavādiśabdānāmapyaśaktijatvāpatteriti | rāddhāntī vadati
&mdash;atrocyate, satyaṃ yadyaśaktijadarśanāt
 asaktijakakvādidarśāt aśaktijatvaṃ
gāvyādeḥ vrūmaḥ vayaṃ tu smṛtito’śaktijatvamupalabhya smṛttermūlasaṃbhavamaśaktijadarśane-
noktavantaḥ |
 asyārthaḥ | smṛtitastāvat gavādiśabdā eva sāsnādimato vācakāḥ na
gāvyādayaḥ | kintu te aśaktijāḥ bhrāntaiḥ vyavahāramārgamavatāryanta iti gamyate | na ceyaṃ
nirmūlā smṛtiḥ, aśaktijenāpi śabdena vyavahāradarśanāditi smṛteḥ nirmūlatvāśaṅkānirākaraṇāyā-
śaktijatvadarśanamupayujyate | yadi hi aśaktijadarśanaṃ na syāt tadā yadyapi smṛtidāḍharyena
smṛtyantaramūlaṃ kalpyeta tathāpi nirmūlataiva syāt | yadā tvaśaktijadarśanamasti tadā anādi-
vyākaraṇaparaṃparāsthitasyānāditvaṃ kalpyate | tena gāvyādīnāmaśaktijatvādādimattā | ko hi
nāmānekaśabdānāmekasminnarthe saṃbhāvyamānāśaktijatvānāṃ sadṛśānāmanāditāṃ kalpayati | na cāndha-
paraṃparādoṣaḥ, anenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvavyākaraṇapraṇetdṛṇāmapi mūlasaṃbhavāt | etadeva vivarītuṃ
codyamāha&mdash;nanu ca tadevedamanumānamukta
maśaktijatvadarśanādapabhraṃśā gāvyādayo’śaktijā iti |
pariharati&mdash;na, tadevoktam, yasmāttathocyamānamanumānaṃ bhavati | tatra ca viruddhādera-

[p. r145]

vasaraḥ | atra punaḥ smṛtermūlābhāvo na śakyate vaktumityetāvatā
 prayojanena
aśaktijadarśanamupanyastam B. adds atha punaraśaktijadarśanamupanyasya |
 kathaṃ punaraśaktijadarśanena mūlābhāvanirākaraṇamityatrāha&mdash;

siddhimapekṣate | asyārthaḥ&mdash;aśaktijatvasaṃbhava-
mātreṇaiva mūlābhāvo nirākriyate na punaḥ gāyavyādīnāmaśaktijatvasiddhayā | yadi hi aśaktijaḥ
śabdo na bhavet tadā sarveṣāmanāditvādvācakatvasthiteriti smṛtirnirmūlā bhavet | yadā tu
aśaktijo’pi śabdaḥ saṃbhavati tadā smṛternirmūlatvaṃ na śakyate vaktum | ato’bhiyukta-

sūtrakārasya | tena sarvābhiyuktavacanānusāreṇa śakyate niyamo’dhyabasātum | evaṃ ca kṛtvā
sadṛśānāṃ śabdānāmekasminnarthe śaktibhedo na kalpitaḥ syāt
 anekaśaktikalpanā-
gauravaṃ parihṛtaṃ bhavati | dṛśyate hyadyatvepyetacchavdasya śabdāntarasmaraṇavyavahitasyārthā-

 tena vṛddhāntarasya gāvīśabdavyavahito’pi
gośabda evārthāvagamaṃ kṛtavān yaddarśanādeva sā  A. bhrāntismṛtirutpannā  gāvī śabda evagośabda eva vācaka iti |
yata eva ca pramāṇāntareṇa anyaśabdoccāraṇepyanyaśabdavivakṣāyā avagamaḥ ata eva vibhakti-

anyathā kathaṃ vyatyayo jñāyeta yadi pramāṇāntareṇa vivakṣā nāvagamyate | tasmādanenāpi
prakāreṇa codanāyāḥ prāmāṇyamākṣeptuṃ na śakyate |

 madhyopanyastāyāḥ kiṃ prayojanam ?|

mavagacchāmaḥ agniśabdasya ṇatvenā B. bhidhānānyamidhānādanyatvaṃ, devāsa iti varṇāntaropajanāta, tmaneti varṇalo-
pena | rāddhāntamāha&mdash;varṇānyatvaṃ hi tat na padānyatvaṃ;
 varṇamātrasya tatrā’nyatvaṃ na
padasya padaṃ hi tadevāvagamyate | nanu ca sphoṭanirākaraṇena varṇātmakameva padaṃ sthāpitaṃ tena
varṇānyatve padānyatvameva pratīyate ityatrāha&mdash;arthapratipattihetutvaṃ hi padatvaṃ tasya caikatvam |

asyārthaḥ&mdash;ekārthapratipattilakṣaṇakāryavaśīkṛtā varṇāḥ kvacit tāvat loke padatāmāpannāḥ teṣu
vedeṣu śrūyamāṇeṣu tadevedaṃ padamiti matirāvirbhavati | tatra yadi kasyacidvarṇasya padasyāpāyo
bhavati varṇāntaraṃ vā’nupraviśati tathāpi tadevedaṃ padaṃ vikṛtamiti pratītirupajāyate nānyaditi |
lokepi bhavatyeveyamavagatiḥ tadeva padaṃ vikṛtaṃ sadanenāśaktyoccaritamiti | aśaktiśca
puruṣadoṣa iti tatparihārāya apramattā avikṛtameva padamuccārayanti | vede tu vakturabhāvānnā-
styuvālabhbhaḥ padaikye cāvagate vākyārtho’pi sa evāvagamyate | tasya padasya tadarthavācitvenāva-
gamāt | lokepi bhavatyeva vivakṣitapadapratyabhijñānenārthāvagatiḥ | yadi paraṃ puruṣasyāśakti-
doṣo bhavati vikṛtapadoccāraṇāt | yatra vā vikṛtapracuravarṇasadbhāvaḥ tatra padapratyabhijñā |
yatra tvalpavarṇoccāraṇaṃ tatrāvayavena samudāyalakṣaṇā loke vikṛtiriti | tathā vede draṣṭavyā |
yathā urūkamityatra kaśabdena kasaḥ medaḥ jai. sū. 9-4-22. kasaśabdalakṣaṇā | loke vikṛtireṣeti viśabdena yathā |

bhāvārthāḥ
 || prathamādhyāye pramāṇalakṣaṇaṃG. pramāṇaṃ vṛttaṃ vṛttaṃ

 G. adds na caitadvijñānaṃśruterhānaṃ yuktam | pratipadaṃ dharmasambandhe parasparasambandhāvagatiḥ
tyaktā syāt kāraṇābhāvādeva | ekakrayākhyakriyānupraveśo hi dravyaguṇayoḥ parasparaṃ sambandhā-

kāmaścātra padārthaḥ na svargaḥ ato’sadevedaṃ pratibhāti | atrocyate | satyaṃ svargakāmaḥ
padārthaḥ
 puruṣapradhānatvādasya śabdasya | sa eva ca puruṣaḥ padārthāntareṇa
 liṅādivācyena
[p. 294]

kāryeṇa niyojyatayā sambadhyate | svargaparastvasau saṃbandhaḥ
 svargabhāvanāparyantaḥ sambandhaḥ
ityaidaṃparyaṃ
 svargasambandhaparyantatvaṃG. paratvaṃ atra bhāṣyakāreṇa darśitam |
 nanu kimarthaṃ svargāśrayaṇe-
nānyotpādakatā prapañcitā | yāvatā sākṣādapūrvamevāśritya kimiti na punaḥ prapañcyata ityatrāha
—dhātvarthasya karaṇatvapradarśanārtham
 | ayamabhiprāyaḥ—yadi ca svargakāmaśabdaḥ svarga-
(kāmanā)bhāvanāparyanto bhavati tadaivāpūrvādhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyenāpūrvaṃ kriyāvyatiriktaṃ kāryaṃ
prasiddhyati | anyathā kriyaiva kāryaṃ syāt | tataśca dhātvarthasya karmataiva bhavet na karaṇatā,
kriyātirikte kārye tatprati phalaṃ ca prati dhātvarthasya karaṇatā siddhyati | nanu ca karaṇatva-
pradarśanasyaiva kiṃ phalamityatrāha—vakṣyati hi sthita etasminnadhikaraṇe guṇavidhirnāmadheya-
miti vicāro bhaviṣyatīti |
 etaduktaṃ bhavati | nāmadheyanirākaraṇārthaṃ bhāvārthagrahaṇamiha kṛtam |
nāmadheyatayā siddhiśca śyenādīnāṃ śabdānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇye sati bhavati | sāmānādhikaraṇya-
siddhiśca yāgasya karaṇatve sati, śyenādiśabdānāṃ karaṇavibhaktiyogitvāt | tena karaṇasidhyā
vinā bhāvārthagrahaṇameva na śakyate vyākhyātuṃ iti karaṇatvapradarśanārthaṃ svargaṃ pratyutpādakatvaṃ
darśitam | kathaṃ punaḥ svargaṃ bhāvayedityevaṃ paratā
 darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetetyevamāde-
rvākyasya | uttaraṃ—yathaivaṃparatā tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 vṛtyarthañcābhisaṃyogāt

ityatra | tasmāttebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeteti nigamitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa | kā punaratra kriyā ?

[p. 295]

uttaraṃ—vidhyarthaḥ
 vidhirūpo yo’rthaḥ | nanu phalasya karaṇaṃ kriyetyuktaṃ
 phalasya ca
yāgaḥ karaṇaṃ, na vidhyarthaḥ
 tasya karaṇatve avākyārthatvaprasaṅgāt | ucyate | yathā vidhyarthaḥ
karaṇaṃ tathā’dhikāralakṣaṇe vakṣyāmaḥ
 na ca vākyārthatvaṃG. vyākhyārthatvaṃ, ātmārthameva karaṇatvāvalambanāt |
taduktam—

ātmasidhyanukūlasya niyojyasya prasiddhaye |
kurvatsvargādikamapi pradhānaṃ kāryameva naḥ || iti |
ihāpi ca bhāṣyakāreṇa te ca yāgadānahomasambaddhāḥ svargasya utpattiṃ vadantīti vadatā
vidhyarthasyaiva karaṇatoktā
 | asya bhāṣyasyāyamarthaḥ—te ca bhāvaśabdāḥ yāgadānahomairabhi-
dheyairabhidhāyakatayā’bhisaṃbaddhāḥ santaḥG. tadviṣayīkaraṇaṃ vidhyarthaṃ vadanti tadviṣayābhidhānadvāreṇāpūrvārthaṃ vadanti | tatra ca vidhyarthe
svargotpattiśabdaṃ prayuñjānena vidhyarthasya svargotpādane karaṇatoktā | utpadyate yayā sotpattiriti
utpattiśabdaḥ karaṇasādhanaḥ | nanu yāgādeḥ karaṇatvābhyupagamāt vidhyarthasya karaṇatvaṃ
nopapadyate ityatrāha—tatsambandhāddhi yāgādeḥ
 karaṇatā karaṇībhūtāpūrvaniṣpādakatayaiva dhātvarthasya
karaṇatvaṃ nānyatheti na doṣaḥ | kuta iti praśno’yaṃ kathaṃ punarniyogārthasya G. karaṇatākaraṇabhāvo’va-
gamyate ityabhiprāyaḥ |
 kāryātmā vidhyarthaḥ phalasiddhau kathaṃ karaṇamityarthaḥ | uttaravādī tu
[p. 296]

eṣahyartho vidhīyate yathā yāgādinā svargakāmaḥ svargaṃ sādhayedityadhikāralakṣaṇasiddhamarthaṃ
darśayati |
 paṣṭhādye hi karmaṇi niyujyamānasya kāminaḥ kāmasiddhau yāgaḥ karaṇamiti kasya
śabdasya vyāpāra iti codayitvā’yamarthaḥ prayatnenoktaḥ | kena sādhayetsvargaṃ yāgādineti |
kimanena granthena pradarśitam
 ? uttaraṃ—saivādhikārikī yuktiḥ
 | yā’sāvadhikāralakṣaṇe
vidhyarthasya karaṇatve yuktiḥ saivoktā’nena granthena | kuta ityāha—svargaṃ sādhayato’dhikāra
iti hi sthāsyāti |
 kāmyasiddhiparyantaḥ kāmino’dhikāraḥ iti vakṣyati | tenāyaṃ bhāṣyārthaḥ
svargakāmo hi svargaṃ sādhayitumicchati | sa kena sādhayetsvargamityasyāmākāṅkṣāyāṃ yajeteti
vidhīyate | yasya ca śabdasyārthena phalaṃ sādhyate tenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā nānyatheti tato’pūrvamavagamyate

ato yastasya vācakaḥ śabdaḥ tato’pūrvaṃ pratīyata iti | pūrvāparavirodhaparihāro’yam

kutaḥ pūrvāparavirodha āśaṅkyate ityatrāha—pūrvaṃ hi niyogātphalamityuktam idānīṃ yāgā-
tphalamiti
 vadato virodhaḥ | taṃ pariharati—tenāpūrvaṃ kṛtvetyavirodhaḥ |
 yāgasyāpi
karaṇatā karaṇībhūtāpūrvasiddhidvāreṇetyānuguṇyānnāsti virodhaḥ | tato’pūrvaṃ gamyata
 iti
sopaskāraṃ bhāṣyaṃ vyācaṣṭe—tato’pūrvameva phalasādhanamiti gamyate phalasādhanamityupaskāraḥ
 |
[p. 297]

yasmādapūrvasiddhiddhāreṇa yāgādeḥ karaṇatā ato’pūrvameva paramakāraṇam | ato yastasya vācakaḥ
tato’pūrva pratīyata iti tena bhāvaśabdā apūrvasya vācakā iti brūma
 iti bhāṣyaṃ pratīkenānubhāṣya
tātparyamāha—ato yastasya vācaka iti nigamanametat
 | upasaṃhāro’yamityarthaḥ | akṣa-
rārthamāha’—yasmādapūrvaṃ sādhayan bhāvārthaḥ phalaṃ niṣpādayati nānyathā kṣaṇikatvāt tena
tadviṣayo vidhyartho liṅgādibhiḥ pratyāyyate tasya ca
 tatpratipādyamānasya viṣayādhīnaprati-
pattittvāt bhāvarthānāṃ ca viṣayabodhakatvāt tato’pūrvaṃ pratīyata ityucyate na punara
pūrvasyāmī1
bhāvaśabdā vācakā iti |
 evaṃ ca darśayan bhāṣyakāraḥ tebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeteti sūtraṃ
vyācaṣṭe-yadbhāvārthebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyata ityucyate tanna sākṣādvācakatvābhiprāyeṇa, kintu viṣayā-
bhidhānadvāreṇeti |

nanu kasmādevaṃ sūtraṃ vyākhyāyana ityatrāha bhāṣyakāraḥ na tu kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣāda-
pūrvasya vācako’stī
ti | tadidaṃ bhāṣyaṃ kecidayuktaṃ manyante | anenaiva hi bhāṣyeṇa sarvathaiva
apūrvasya vācakaḥ śabdo nāstītyucyate | taccāyuktam | liṅādīnāṃ tadvācakatvāt ityā-
śaṅkyāha—na tu kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stīti bhāvārthābhiprāyametat |

[p. 298]

bhāvārthān śabdānabhipretya vācakatvaniṣedhaḥ na śabdamātrābhiprāyeṇa | tadāha—na punarliṅādi-
śabdo’pi niyogaṃ nācaṣṭe
 | nanu yadyapi sūtre tebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyata iti bhāvārthānāmeva
niyogābhidhāyakatā’vasīyate tathāpi pramāṇābhāvānneyamāśaṅkocitā yannirākaraṇāyedamucyate na tu
kaścicchabdaḥ sākṣādapūrvasya vācako’stīti | tadāha---kathaṃ punarbhāvārthe vācakatvāśaṅkā
 ?
uttaraṃ---kevalasyāprayogāt niyogaśabdasya
 liṅādeḥ | yadi bhāvārthavyatirekeṇa keva-
lena prayuktena liṅādinā vidhyartho’vasīyateG. vidhīyate tadā bhāvārthevācakatvāśaṅkā na syāt | na ca
kevalasya prayogo’sti nityaṃ prakṛtiparasyaiva G. niyogaprayogaviyamāt | tiḍantasyaiva prayogāt
tiṅanta eva prayujyate bhāvārtho’pi na śuddhaḥ | tena yāvatprayogānuvartinyeva vācakateti
bhrāntānāṃ
 yāvadeva prakṛtipratyayātmakaṃ padaṃ prayujyate tadanusāryeva vācakatvaṃ G. tathaivatasyaiva
vācakatvamiti bhrāntānāṃ arthāvagamaṃ muktvā
 anyena prakāreṇa prayogeyattā na siddhyati

yadi kevalasyāpi vācakatvaṃ tadā kimiti kevalo’pi na prayujyate | na hyanyonyārthāvagamaṃ
muktvā prayogaparimāṇopāyaḥ yāvatā hyarthaḥ pratyāyyate tāvadeva prayujyata itīdaṃ mataṃ nirākartuma-
vācaka ityuktam
 | nanu yadi prayogektāyā anyathānupapattyā vācakatvamāśaṅkitaṃ tarhi na yuktaṃ
nirākartum
 ekena granthenāha-kiṃ vā prayogeyattā anyathā sidhyati
 yenaG.  yena vā yatprayo yāvatprayogānuvartinī
vācakatā nirākiyate | pariharati---G. arthāvagamo’pi bhāvādaarthāvagamo vā vibhāgādavagamyamānaḥ kimapahnotuṃ

[p. 299]

śakyate |
 liṅādipratyayamantareṇaG. prathamatareṇa juhoti yajatītyādau bhāvārthāḥ pratīyante prakṛtyantarapare ca
liṅādau prayukte’pūrvamavagamyata ityanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ liṅāderevāpūrvavācakatvam | dhātostu
bhāvārthābhidhāyakatvamavagamyamānaṃ na śakyate’pahnotum | tena bhāvārthānāṃ nāpūrvaṃ prati vācakatā |
yadi vibhāgena vācakatvaṃ kiṃ tarhi prayoganiyame kāraṇaṃ ?
 kimiti kevalo liṅādiśabdo
na prayujyate ? uttaram---prayoga eva
 prayoganiyame kāraṇam | nanu prayoga eva kāraṇe pṛṣṭe
prayoga eva heturiti na yuktamuttaramityatrāha---vācakatvasyeva prayogasyāpyanāditoktā
 yathā
vācakatvaṃ śabdānāṃ anāditayā, prayogopi vṛddhavyavahāraparamparayā, vimalāñjane
’nāditvasya jñāpi-
tattvāt | tenottareṣāṃ pusāṃ pūrvapūrvapuruṣaprayoga eva niyame kāraṇam | yo hi yathābhūtaḥ
prayujyamāno vācako dṛṣṭaḥ śabdaḥ sa tathābhūta eva prayujyata iti niyamasiddhiḥ | tasmāt
sūktaṃ na bhāvaśabdena niyogo’rtho’bhidhīyata iti | bhāvārthaiḥ kimapi bhāvayitavya-
mityevamādimiravaibhaktikasaṃbandhaṃ
 phalaniyogabhāvārthānāṃ darśayati | yasmādayamavaibhaktikaḥ
saṃbandhaḥ tasmādayamavainiyogika iti siddham | anyathā hi yadyevaṃ
 na pradarśyate tadā
[p. 300]

viniyogābhāvāt vibhaktavidhānānusārī kaścit bādaririvāsaṃbandhaṃ manyate |
 nanu
manyatāṃ nāma tathāpīha kā kṣatirityatrāha---tataśca sthita etasminnadhikaraṇe guṇavidhirnāmadheya-
miti vicāro bhaviṣyatīti na śakyate vaktum |
 na hi karaṇatāmantareṇodbhidādīnāṃ
nāmatetyuktametatpurastāt | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ
 kena punaḥ kāraṇenetyarthaḥ viniyojakalakṣaṇo’yaṃ

saṃbandho na bhavati | uttaraṃ---viniyojakānāṃ śrutyādīnāmabhāvāt | kathamabhāvaḥ ?
 ayamāśayaḥ
pṛcchakasya---yajyādiśrutireva śrutiḥ kasmānna bhavati | uttaraṃ---saṃbandhasyānavagamāt
yadi
 yajyādiśrutisamanantaramaidamarthyalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho’vagamyate tadā bhavet śruti-
saṃbhavaḥ | na cāsāvavagamyate | sāmānyenaiva saṃbandhāvagamo sabandhenā nāstītyuktamiti matvā coda-
yati---nanu cāyamasti saṃbandhaḥ yajeta svargakāma iti |
 pariharati---bāḍhamasti na puna-
raidamarthyalakṣaṇaḥ kiṃ tarhi adhikāralakṣaṇaḥ
